/**
 * PENGENDALI LOADING BAR TOOLBAR RAMPING (PENGGANTI OVERLAY HITAM KAKU)
 * Menggunakan Indeterminate Shimmer Gradient Bar di Topbar + Teks Status Dinamis
 */
window.showGlobalLoader = function(msg) {
  var barContainer = document.getElementById('topbarProgressContainer');
  var statusBadge = document.getElementById('toolbarProgressStatus');
  var statusText = document.getElementById('toolbarProgressStatusText');

  if (barContainer) {
    barContainer.style.display = 'block';
  }
  if (statusBadge && statusText) {
    statusText.textContent = msg || 'Menyiapkan data...';
    statusBadge.style.display = 'inline-flex';
    statusBadge.style.opacity = '1';
  }
};

window.hideGlobalLoader = function(finishMsg) {
  var barContainer = document.getElementById('topbarProgressContainer');
  var statusBadge = document.getElementById('toolbarProgressStatus');
  var statusText = document.getElementById('toolbarProgressStatusText');

  if (statusBadge && statusText) {
    statusText.textContent = finishMsg || 'Selesai 100%';
    // ZettBOT Feature: Micro-Status Fade-Out otomatis dalam 0.3 detik (300ms)
    setTimeout(function() {
      statusBadge.style.opacity = '0';
      setTimeout(function() {
        statusBadge.style.display = 'none';
        if (barContainer) barContainer.style.display = 'none';
      }, 300);
    }, 300);
  } else if (barContainer) {
    barContainer.style.display = 'none';
  }
};

/**
 * HELPER ANIMASI SPINNER PADA TOMBOL LOGIN & SUBMIT (.btn-spinner)
 */
window.setButtonSpinner = function(btnId, isLoading, defaultHtml, loadingText) {
  var btn = document.getElementById(btnId);
  if (!btn) return;
  if (isLoading) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    if (!btn.getAttribute('data-original-html')) {
      btn.setAttribute('data-original-html', btn.innerHTML);
    }
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="btn-spinner"></span> <span>' + (loadingText || 'Memverifikasi...') + '</span>';
  } else {
    btn.disabled = false;
    var orig = btn.getAttribute('data-original-html') || defaultHtml;
    if (orig) btn.innerHTML = orig;
  }
};

/**
 * PENGAMBILAN LOKASI GPS OTOMATIS SAAT APLIKASI PERTAMA KALI DIBUKA
 */
window.triggerImmediateLocationFetch = function() {
  if (navigator && navigator.geolocation) {
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      function(pos) {
        window.currentLat = pos.coords.latitude;
        window.currentLng = pos.coords.longitude;
        if (window.currentUser && typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.updateHeartbeat === 'function') {
          google.script.run.updateHeartbeat(window.currentUser, window.currentLat, window.currentLng);
        }
      },
      function(err) {
        // Silent fallback: biarkan pengguna membuka izin lokasi nanti
      },
      { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000, maximumAge: 60000 }
    );
  }
};

/**
 * ZETTBOT FEATURE 2: ONE-CLICK SUMMARY FILTER & RESET POINT BADGE
 */
window.filterByPointFromSummary = function(pointName) {
  if (!pointName || pointName === '-' || pointName.toLowerCase() === 'total') return;
  var selPt = document.getElementById('filterPoint');
  var badgeWrapper = document.getElementById('activePointFilterBadgeWrapper');
  var badgeName = document.getElementById('activePointFilterName');

  if (selPt) {
    selPt.value = pointName;
  }
  if (badgeWrapper && badgeName) {
    badgeName.textContent = pointName;
    badgeWrapper.style.display = 'block';
  }
  window.applyFilter();

  var tblBox = document.getElementById('tableBox');
  if (tblBox) {
    tblBox.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  }
};

window.resetPointFilter = function() {
  var selPt = document.getElementById('filterPoint');
  var badgeWrapper = document.getElementById('activePointFilterBadgeWrapper');
  if (selPt) selPt.value = '';
  if (badgeWrapper) badgeWrapper.style.display = 'none';
  window.applyFilter();
};

window.togglePointSummaryCollapse = function() {
  var container = document.getElementById('summaryPointTableContainer');
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnCollapsePointSummary');
  if (!container || !btn) return;
  var isHidden = (container.style.display === 'none');
  container.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
  btn.textContent = isHidden ? '[-] Ciutkan' : '[+] Buka Summary';
};

/**
 * DRAG & DROP & PARSER FILE MASTER LEADS (WPS / EXCEL / CSV) MURNI CLIENT-SIDE
 * Mendukung header dinamis murni sesuai hasil berkas asli yang diunggah
 */
window.handleDragOver = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  event.stopPropagation();
  var box = document.getElementById('uploadBahanKunjunganBox');
  if (box) box.classList.add('dragover');
};

window.handleDragLeave = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  event.stopPropagation();
  var box = document.getElementById('uploadBahanKunjunganBox');
  if (box) box.classList.remove('dragover');
};

window.handleFileDrop = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  event.stopPropagation();
  var box = document.getElementById('uploadBahanKunjunganBox');
  if (box) box.classList.remove('dragover');

  var dt = event.dataTransfer;
  if (dt && dt.files && dt.files.length > 0) {
    window.processBahanKunjunganFile(dt.files[0]);
  }
};

window.handleBahanKunjunganFileSelected = function(event) {
  var file = event.target.files[0];
  if (file) {
    window.processBahanKunjunganFile(file);
  }
};

window.processBahanKunjunganFile = function(file) {
  if (!file) return;

  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  if (role !== '1-RM' && role !== '2-AM' && role !== 'Editor' && role !== 'Admin') {
    window.showLargeAlert('Akses Ditolak', 'Hanya Role 1-RM dan 2-AM yang diizinkan mengunggah berkas!', false);
    return;
  }

  var fileNameEl = document.getElementById('uploadBahanKunjunganFileName');
  if (fileNameEl) fileNameEl.textContent = 'Memproses berkas: ' + file.name + '...';

  window.showGlobalLoader('Membaca berkas Excel/WPS lokal...');

  var reader = new FileReader();
  var isBinary = file.name.match(/\.(xlsx|xls)$/i);

  reader.onload = function(e) {
    try {
      var rows = [], headers = [];

      if (isBinary && typeof XLSX !== 'undefined') {
        var data = new Uint8Array(e.target.result);
        var workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'array' });
        var firstSheetName = workbook.SheetNames[0];
        var worksheet = workbook.Sheets[firstSheetName];
        var jsonResult = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(worksheet, { header: 1, defval: '' });

        if (jsonResult && jsonResult.length > 1) {
          headers = jsonResult[0].map(function(h) { return String(h || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
          for (var r = 1; r < jsonResult.length; r++) {
            var rowArr = jsonResult[r];
            var rowObj = {};
            var hasVal = false;
            for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
              var val = rowArr[c] !== undefined ? rowArr[c] : '';
              if (String(val).trim() !== '') hasVal = true;
              rowObj[headers[c]] = val;
              rowObj['_raw_' + c] = val;
            }
            if (hasVal) rows.push(rowObj);
          }
        }
      } else {
        var content = e.target.result;
        var parsed = window.parseCsvOrTextData(content);
        rows = parsed.rows || [];
        headers = parsed.headers || [];
      }

      window.hideGlobalLoader('100% Selesai Dibaca');

      if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {
        window.showLargeAlert('Gagal', 'Berkas kosong atau tidak ada baris data yang valid.', false);
        if (fileNameEl) fileNameEl.textContent = '';
        return;
      }

      // Terapkan kolom dinamis murni dari file yang diunggah
      window.currentHeaders = headers;
      window.allData = rows;
      window.filteredData = rows;
      window.currentPage = 1;
      
      if (typeof window.renderTable === 'function') window.renderTable();
      if (typeof window.renderPointSummaryTable === 'function') window.renderPointSummaryTable(window.currentKey, rows);

      window.showGlobalLoader('Menyimpan data Master Leads ke database...');

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.uploadBahanKunjungan === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          window.hideGlobalLoader('Tersimpan di Cloud');
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            window.showLargeAlert('Berhasil Diunggah', res.message, true);
            if (fileNameEl) fileNameEl.textContent = '✓ ' + file.name + ' (' + rows.length + ' baris • ' + headers.length + ' kolom)';
            if (res.audit) {
              var banner = document.getElementById('auditTrailBanner');
              var uInfo = document.getElementById('auditUploaderInfo');
              var tBadge = document.getElementById('auditTimestampBadge');
              if (banner && uInfo && tBadge) {
                uInfo.textContent = res.audit.uploaderRole + ' ' + res.audit.uploaderName + ' (' + res.audit.fileName + ')';
                tBadge.textContent = res.audit.timestampWITA;
                banner.style.display = 'flex';
              }
            }
            window.loadCurrentData(true);
          } else {
            window.showLargeAlert('Gagal Unggah', (res ? res.message : 'Terjadi kendala sistem'), false);
            if (fileNameEl) fileNameEl.textContent = '';
          }
        }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
          window.hideGlobalLoader();
          window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
          if (fileNameEl) fileNameEl.textContent = '';
        }).uploadBahanKunjungan({
          menuType: 'Master Leads',
          fileName: file.name,
          rowsData: rows,
          headers: headers
        }, window.currentUser);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.showLargeAlert('Format File Error', 'Gagal memproses berkas Excel/CSV: ' + err.message, false);
      if (fileNameEl) fileNameEl.textContent = '';
    }
  };

  if (isBinary) {
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  } else {
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }
};

window.parseCsvOrTextData = function(text) {
  var lines = text.split(/\r\n|\n/);
  if (lines.length < 2) return { headers: [], rows: [] };
  
  var parseLine = function(line) {
    var delimiter = line.indexOf(';') !== -1 ? ';' : ',';
    var entries = [];
    var current = '';
    var inQuotes = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
      var c = line[i];
      if (c === '"') { inQuotes = !inQuotes; }
      else if (c === delimiter && !inQuotes) {
        entries.push(current.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
        current = '';
      } else { current += c; }
    }
    entries.push(current.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
    return entries;
  };

  var headers = parseLine(lines[0]).filter(Boolean);
  var rows = [];

  for (var i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
    var raw = lines[i].trim();
    if (!raw) continue;
    var rowValues = parseLine(raw);
    var rowObj = {};
    var hasVal = false;
    for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
      var cell = rowValues[c] !== undefined ? rowValues[c] : '';
      if (cell !== '') hasVal = true;
      rowObj[headers[c]] = cell;
      rowObj['_raw_' + c] = cell;
    }
    if (hasVal) rows.push(rowObj);
  }

  return { headers: headers, rows: rows };
};

/**
 * PELACAKAN PETA SATELIT 3D ISOMETRIC (LEAFLET CSS TRANSFORM)
 */
window.toggle3DMap = function() {
  window.is3DMapActive = !window.is3DMapActive;
  var mapBox = document.getElementById('trackingMapBox');
  var btnToggle = document.getElementById('btnToggle3D');
  if (!mapBox || !btnToggle) return;

  if (window.is3DMapActive) {
    mapBox.classList.add('map-3d-view');
    btnToggle.classList.add('active');
    btnToggle.innerHTML = '<span>🧊</span> <span>Tampilan 3D Aktif</span>';
  } else {
    mapBox.classList.remove('map-3d-view');
    btnToggle.classList.remove('active');
    btnToggle.innerHTML = '<span>🗺️</span> <span>Tampilan Datar (2D)</span>';
  }

  setTimeout(function() {
    if (window.trackingMap) window.trackingMap.invalidateSize();
  }, 350);
};

window.toggleMapLayerType = function() {
  window.isSatelliteLayer = !window.isSatelliteLayer;
  var btn = document.getElementById('mapLayerLabel');
  if (btn) btn.textContent = window.isSatelliteLayer ? 'Mode Satelit Google' : 'Mode Peta Jalan Standard';

  if (!window.trackingMap || typeof L === 'undefined') return;

  if (window.currentActiveTileLayer) {
    window.trackingMap.removeLayer(window.currentActiveTileLayer);
  }

  var tileUrl = window.isSatelliteLayer ?
    'https://mt1.google.com/vt/lyrs=y&x={x}&y={y}&z={z}' :
    'https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png';

  window.currentActiveTileLayer = L.tileLayer(tileUrl, { maxZoom: 20 }).addTo(window.trackingMap);
};

window.playChimeSound = function(type) {
  if (!window.soundNotificationEnabled) return;
  try {
    var AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AudioCtx) return;
    var ctx = new AudioCtx();
    var osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    var gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(ctx.destination);

    if (type === 'chat') {
      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, ctx.currentTime);
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime + 0.1);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.18, ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.38);
      osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.38);
    } else {
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, ctx.currentTime);
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, ctx.currentTime + 0.12);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.14, ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.4);
      osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.4);
    }
  } catch(e) {}
};

window.openSettingsModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('settingsModal');
  if (!modal) return;
  var sToggle = document.getElementById('settingSoundToggle');
  var tToggle = document.getElementById('settingToastToggle');
  if (sToggle) sToggle.checked = window.soundNotificationEnabled;
  if (tToggle) tToggle.checked = window.toastNotificationEnabled;

  var isDark = document.body.classList.contains('dark-mode');
  var bLight = document.getElementById('themeBtnLight');
  var bDark = document.getElementById('themeBtnDark');
  if (bLight && bDark) {
    if (isDark) { bDark.classList.add('active'); bLight.classList.remove('active'); }
    else { bLight.classList.add('active'); bDark.classList.remove('active'); }
  }

  var bId = document.getElementById('langBtnId');
  var bEn = document.getElementById('langBtnEn');
  if (bId && bEn) {
    if (window.currentLang === 'en') { bEn.classList.add('active'); bId.classList.remove('active'); }
    else { bId.classList.add('active'); bEn.classList.remove('active'); }
  }
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeSettingsModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('settingsModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.onSettingSoundToggleChange = function(enabled) {
  window.soundNotificationEnabled = !!enabled;
  localStorage.setItem('sound_notif_enabled', window.soundNotificationEnabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  if (window.soundNotificationEnabled) window.playChimeSound('chat');
  window.showToast(window.soundNotificationEnabled ? '🔔 Notifikasi Suara Diaktifkan' : '🔕 Notifikasi Suara Dimatikan');
};

window.onSettingToastToggleChange = function(enabled) {
  window.toastNotificationEnabled = !!enabled;
  localStorage.setItem('toast_notif_enabled', window.toastNotificationEnabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  window.showToast(window.toastNotificationEnabled ? '💬 Pop-up Notifikasi Diaktifkan' : '📴 Pop-up Notifikasi Dimatikan');
};

window.setThemeMode = function(mode) {
  var bLight = document.getElementById('themeBtnLight');
  var bDark = document.getElementById('themeBtnDark');
  if (mode === 'dark') {
    document.body.classList.add('dark-mode');
    localStorage.setItem('zettbos_theme', 'dark');
    if (bDark) bDark.classList.add('active');
    if (bLight) bLight.classList.remove('active');
  } else {
    document.body.classList.remove('dark-mode');
    localStorage.setItem('zettbos_theme', 'light');
    if (bLight) bLight.classList.add('active');
    if (bDark) bDark.classList.remove('active');
  }
};

window.setLanguage = function(lang) {
  window.currentLang = (lang === 'en') ? 'en' : 'id';
  localStorage.setItem('zettbos_lang', window.currentLang);
  var bId = document.getElementById('langBtnId');
  var bEn = document.getElementById('langBtnEn');
  if (bId && bEn) {
    if (window.currentLang === 'en') { bEn.classList.add('active'); bId.classList.remove('active'); }
    else { bId.classList.add('active'); bEn.classList.remove('active'); }
  }
  window.applyLanguageTranslations();
  window.showToast(window.currentLang === 'en' ? 'Language switched to English' : 'Bahasa dialihkan ke Indonesia');
};

window.applyLanguageTranslations = function() {
  var lang = window.currentLang || 'id';
  var dict = window.i18nDict[lang] || window.i18nDict['id'];

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(function(el) {
    var key = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
    if (dict[key]) el.textContent = dict[key];
  });
};

window.updateRefreshTimeAgoDisplay = function() {
  var el = document.getElementById('refreshTimeAgo');
  if (!el) return;
  var diffSec = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - window.lastDataRefreshTimestamp) / 1000));
  if (diffSec < 10) {
    el.textContent = '(Baru saja)';
  } else if (diffSec < 60) {
    el.textContent = '(Diperbarui ' + diffSec + ' detik lalu)';
  } else {
    var diffMin = Math.floor(diffSec / 60);
    el.textContent = '(Diperbarui ' + diffMin + ' menit lalu)';
  }
};

window.startRefreshTimeTracker = function() {
  window.lastDataRefreshTimestamp = Date.now();
  window.updateRefreshTimeAgoDisplay();
  if (window.refreshTimeTrackerInterval) clearInterval(window.refreshTimeTrackerInterval);
  window.refreshTimeTrackerInterval = setInterval(window.updateRefreshTimeAgoDisplay, 10000);
};

window.highlightCurrentDayInKpWeek = function() {
  var dayNames = ['Minggu', 'Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'];
  var todayIdx = new Date().getDay();
  var todayName = dayNames[todayIdx];

  ['Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'].forEach(function(day) {
    var dotEl = document.getElementById('dotToday' + day);
    if (dotEl) {
      dotEl.style.display = (day === todayName) ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
    }
  });
};

window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl = function(url) {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return '';
  url = url.trim();
  if (url === '-' || url === '') return '';

  if (url.indexOf('lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/') !== -1) return url;

  if (url.indexOf('drive.google.com') !== -1 || url.indexOf('docs.google.com') !== -1) {
    var idMatch = url.match(/\/file\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,})/) || 
                  url.match(/id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,})/) || 
                  url.match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,})/);
    if (idMatch && idMatch[1]) {
      var lower = url.toLowerCase();
      var isMedia = lower.indexOf('video') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('audio') !== -1 ||
                    lower.indexOf('.mp4') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('.webm') !== -1 ||
                    lower.indexOf('.mov') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('.3gp') !== -1 ||
                    lower.indexOf('.mp3') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('.wav') !== -1 ||
                    lower.indexOf('.m4a') !== -1;
      if (isMedia) {
        return 'https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + idMatch[1] + '/preview';
      }
      return 'https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/' + idMatch[1];
    }
  }
  return url;
};

window.formatWhatsAppNumber = function(phone) {
  if (!phone && phone !== 0) return '';
  var clean = String(phone).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').trim();
  if (!clean) return '';
  if (clean.indexOf('08') === 0) {
    clean = '62' + clean.substring(1);
  } else if (clean.indexOf('8') === 0 && clean.indexOf('628') !== 0) {
    clean = '62' + clean;
  } else if (clean.indexOf('6208') === 0) {
    clean = '62' + clean.substring(3);
  }
  return clean;
};

window.isValidId = function(idVal) {
  if (idVal === null || idVal === undefined) return false;
  var str = String(idVal).trim().replace(/['"`\s]/g, '');
  if (!str || str === '-' || str === 'null' || str === 'undefined' || str === '0' || str === '00' || str === '000') return false;
  var numericOnly = str.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
  if (!numericOnly) return false;
  var num = parseInt(numericOnly, 10);
  return !isNaN(num) && num > 0;
};

window.normalizeNumericId = function(rawId) {
  if (!window.isValidId(rawId)) return '';
  var cleanStr = String(rawId).trim().replace(/['"`\s]/g, '');
  return cleanStr.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
};

window.calculateNameSimilarity = function(str1, str2) {
  var s1 = String(str1 || '').toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  var s2 = String(str2 || '').toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  if (!s1 || !s2) return 0;
  if (s1 === s2) return 1.0;

  var longer = s1.length > s2.length ? s1 : s2;
  var shorter = s1.length > s2.length ? s2 : s1;
  var longerLength = longer.length;
  if (longerLength === 0) return 1.0;

  var costs = [];
  for (var i = 0; i <= longer.length; i++) {
    var lastValue = i;
    for (var j = 0; j <= shorter.length; j++) {
      if (i === 0) costs[j] = j;
      else {
        if (j > 0) {
          var newValue = costs[j - 1];
          if (longer.charAt(i - 1) !== shorter.charAt(j - 1)) {
            newValue = Math.min(Math.min(newValue, lastValue), costs[j]) + 1;
          }
          costs[j - 1] = lastValue;
          lastValue = newValue;
        }
      }
    }
    if (i > 0) costs[shorter.length] = lastValue;
  }
  return (longerLength - costs[shorter.length]) / parseFloat(longerLength);
};

window.numVal = function(val) {
  if (val === null || val === undefined) return 0;
  if (typeof val === 'number') return val;
  var str = String(val).trim();
  str = str.replace(/[^-0-9.,]/g, '');
  if (!str) return 0;

  var lastComma = str.lastIndexOf(',');
  var lastDot = str.lastIndexOf('.');

  if (lastComma > -1 && lastDot > -1) {
    if (lastComma > lastDot) { str = str.replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.'); } 
    else { str = str.replace(/,/g, ''); }
  } else if (lastComma > -1) {
    if (str.split(',').length > 2 || (str.length - lastComma === 4 && str.split(',')[1].length === 3)) {
      str = str.replace(/,/g, '');
    } else {
      str = str.replace(',', '.');
    }
  } else if (lastDot > -1) {
    if (str.split('.').length > 2 || (str.length - lastDot === 4 && str.split('.')[1].length === 3)) {
      str = str.replace(/\./g, '');
    }
  }
  var num = parseFloat(str);
  return isNaN(num) ? 0 : num;
};

window.parseDateStr = function(str) {
  if (!str) return null;
  if (str instanceof Date) return isNaN(str.getTime()) ? null : str;
  str = String(str).trim();
  var datePart = str.split(' ')[0];

  if (datePart.indexOf('/') !== -1) {
    var parts = datePart.split('/');
    if (parts.length === 3) {
      var p0 = parseInt(parts[0], 10);
      var p1 = parseInt(parts[1], 10);
      var p2 = parseInt(parts[2], 10);
      var y, m, d;
      if (parts[0].length === 4) {
        y = p0; m = p1 - 1; d = p2;
      } else {
        d = p0; m = p1 - 1; y = p2;
        if (p1 > 12 && p0 <= 12) {
          m = p0 - 1; d = p1;
        }
      }
      var dt = new Date(y, m, d);
      return isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
    }
  } else if (datePart.indexOf('-') !== -1) {
    var p2 = datePart.split('-');
    if (p2.length === 3) {
      if (p2[0].length === 4) {
        var dt2 = new Date(parseInt(p2[0], 10), parseInt(p2[1], 10) - 1, parseInt(p2[2], 10));
        return isNaN(dt2.getTime()) ? null : dt2;
      } else {
        var dt3 = new Date(parseInt(p2[2], 10), parseInt(p2[1], 10) - 1, parseInt(p2[0], 10));
        return isNaN(dt3.getTime()) ? null : dt3;
      }
    }
  }
  var parsed = new Date(str);
  return isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed;
};

window.isToday = function(str) {
  var d = window.parseDateStr(str);
  if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return false;
  var today = new Date();
  return d.getDate() === today.getDate() && 
         d.getMonth() === today.getMonth() && 
         d.getFullYear() === today.getFullYear();
};

};

window.getCellValue = function(row, key, rawIdx) {
  if (!row) return '';
  if (rawIdx !== undefined && rawIdx !== null && row['_raw_' + rawIdx] !== undefined && row['_raw_' + rawIdx] !== null && row['_raw_' + rawIdx] !== '') {
    return row['_raw_' + rawIdx];
  }
  if (!key) return '';
  if (row[key] !== undefined && row[key] !== null && row[key] !== '') return row[key];
  var keyLower = String(key).toLowerCase();
  if (row[keyLower] !== undefined && row[keyLower] !== null && row[keyLower] !== '') return row[keyLower];
  
  var keyNorm = keyLower.replace(/%/g, 'persen').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  if (row[keyNorm] !== undefined && row[keyNorm] !== null && row[keyNorm] !== '') return row[keyNorm];
  
  for (var rk in row) {
    if (rk.indexOf('_raw_') === 0) continue;
    var rkNorm = rk.toLowerCase().replace(/%/g, 'persen').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
    if (rkNorm === keyNorm && row[rk] !== undefined && row[rk] !== null && row[rk] !== '') return row[rk];
  }

  var aliasMap = {
    'nik': ['nik', 'nikbp', 'nik_bp', 'nik bp'],
    'bpbm': ['bpbm', 'bp', 'namabp', 'namabpbm', 'nama', 'bp/bm', 'nama bp', 'nama bp/bm', 'bp_uk', 'bpuk', 'agent_fullname'],

    'area': ['area'],

    'group_name': ['group_name', 'groupname', 'group', 'nama_group'],
    'aktivitas': ['aktivitas', 'aktifitas', 'aktivitasregional', 'kegiatan', 'jenisaktivitas'],
    'targetntb': ['targetntb', 'target_ntb'],
    'targetetb': ['targetetb', 'target_etb'],
    'actualntb': ['actualntb', 'actual_ntb'],
    'actualetb': ['actualetb', 'actual_etb'],
    'jumlahmitra': ['jumlahmitra', 'jumlah_mitra', 'borrower'],
    'totaldisb': ['totaldisb', 'total_disb', 'actualdisb', 'actualtotal'],
    'totalplafond': ['totalplafond', 'total_plafond', 'plafond', 'principal_amount', 'actualdisb'],
    'total': ['total'],
    'target': ['target'],
    'targettotal': ['targettotal', 'target_total'],
    'ratarataplafonddicairkan': ['ratarataplafonddicairkan', 'rataplafonddicairkan'],

    'achievmentetb': ['achievmentetb', 'achievementetb'],

  if (aliasMap[keyNorm]) {
    var aliases = aliasMap[keyNorm];
    for (var i = 0; i < aliases.length; i++) {
      var al = aliases[i];
      if (row[al] !== undefined && row[al] !== null && row[al] !== '') return row[al];
      for (var rk2 in row) {
        if (rk2.indexOf('_raw_') === 0) continue;
        var rk2Norm = rk2.toLowerCase().replace(/%/g, 'persen').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
        if (rk2Norm === al && row[rk2] !== undefined && row[rk2] !== null && row[rk2] !== '') return row[rk2];
      }
    }
  }

  if (keyNorm === 'aktivitas' && window.currentKey !== 'dashboard') {
    return window.getDefaultActivityName(window.currentKey);
  }

  return '';
};

window.showToast = function(msg) {
  var container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
  if (!container) return;
  var t = document.createElement('div');
  t.className = 'custom-toast';
  t.innerHTML = '<span>🔔</span><span>' + msg + '</span>';
  container.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(function() { if (t.parentNode) t.parentNode.removeChild(t); }, 3500);
};

window.showLargeAlert = function(title, msg, isSuccess) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('largeAlertModal');
  var icon = document.getElementById('alertIcon');
  var t = document.getElementById('alertTitle');
  var m = document.getElementById('alertMessage');
  if (!modal) return;
  if (icon) {
    icon.className = 'large-alert-icon ' + (isSuccess ? 'success' : 'error');
    icon.textContent = isSuccess ? '✓' : '✕';
  }
  if (t) t.textContent = title || 'Notifikasi Sistem';
  if (m) m.innerHTML = msg || '';
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeLargeAlert = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('largeAlertModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.showCustomConfirm = function(title, msg, callback) {
  window.confirmCallback = callback;
  var cModal = document.getElementById('confirmModal');
  var cTitle = document.getElementById('confirmTitle');
  var cMsg = document.getElementById('confirmMessage');
  if (cTitle) cTitle.textContent = title || 'Konfirmasi';
  if (cMsg) cMsg.textContent = msg || 'Apakah Anda yakin?';
  if (cModal) cModal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeCustomConfirm = function(confirmed) {
  var cModal = document.getElementById('confirmModal');
  if (cModal) cModal.style.display = 'none';
  if (confirmed && typeof window.confirmCallback === 'function') window.confirmCallback();
  window.confirmCallback = null;
};

window.requestLogout = function() {
  window.showCustomConfirm(
    'Konfirmasi Logout',
    'Apakah Anda yakin ingin keluar dari akun Anda?',
    function() {
      if (typeof window.handleLogout === 'function') {
        window.handleLogout();
      }
    }
  );
};

window.checkSessionExpiry = function() {
  if (!window.currentUser) return true;
  var loginTs = window.currentUser.loginTimestamp || 0;
  var now = Date.now();
  if (loginTs && (now - loginTs > window.SESSION_MAX_DURATION)) {
    window.showLargeAlert('Sesi Kedaluwarsa', 'Sesi login Anda telah berakhir (3 jam). Silakan login kembali demi keamanan data.', false);
    if (typeof window.handleLogout === 'function') window.handleLogout();
    setTimeout(function() { if (typeof window.openAuthModal === 'function') window.openAuthModal('login'); }, 600);
    return false;
  }
  return true;
};

window.toggleKpiSubmenu = function() {
  var container = document.getElementById('kpiSubmenuContainer');
  var arrow = document.getElementById('kpiAccordionArrow');
  if (container) {
    if (container.style.display === 'none' || container.style.display === '') {
      container.style.display = 'flex';
      if (arrow) arrow.textContent = '▲';
    } else {
      container.style.display = 'none';
      if (arrow) arrow.textContent = '▼';
    }
  }
};

window.filterBahanKunjunganStatus = function(status) {
  window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus = status || 'all';
  document.querySelectorAll('.btn-shortcut-status').forEach(function(btn) {
    if (btn.getAttribute('data-status') === window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus) {
      btn.classList.add('active');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('active');
    }
  });
  window.applyFilter();
};

window.openMenu = function(key, elem) {
  if (!window.checkSessionExpiry()) return;

  window.currentKey = key;
  window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly = false;
  window.validationQuickFilterState = 'all';
  window.isDisburseTodayFilter = false;
  window.gdQuickFilterState = 'all';
  window.currentGdWeek = '';
  window.matrixStatusFilter = 'all';
  window.currentKpWeekDay = 'all';
  window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus = 'all';

  // Efek transisi masuk dari kiri halus saat menu dibuka
  var mainSection = document.getElementById('mainContentSection');
  if (mainSection) {
    mainSection.classList.remove('menu-transition-in');
    void mainSection.offsetWidth; // trigger reflow
    mainSection.classList.add('menu-transition-in');
  }

  var badgeWrapper = document.getElementById('activePointFilterBadgeWrapper');
  if (badgeWrapper) badgeWrapper.style.display = 'none';
  
  var btnDisbToday = document.getElementById('btnDisbToday');
  if (btnDisbToday) btnDisbToday.classList.remove('active');

  var btnValAll = document.getElementById('btnValAll');
  var btnValSudah = document.getElementById('btnValSudah');
  var btnValBelum = document.getElementById('btnValBelum');
  var btnValGagal = document.getElementById('btnValGagal');
  if (btnValAll) btnValAll.className = 'btn-toggle-val active-all';
  if (btnValSudah) btnValSudah.className = 'btn-toggle-val';
  if (btnValBelum) btnValBelum.className = 'btn-toggle-val';
  if (btnValGagal) btnValGagal.className = 'btn-toggle-val';

  document.querySelectorAll('.menu').forEach(function(m) { m.classList.remove('active'); });
  if (elem) elem.classList.add('active');

  var absensiBox = document.getElementById('absensiBox');
  var laporanAbsensiBox = document.getElementById('laporanAbsensiBox');
  var trackingMapBox = document.getElementById('trackingMapBox');
  var statsBox = document.getElementById('stats');
  var kpiHeroContainer = document.getElementById('kpiScoreHeroContainer');
  var visualChartBox = document.getElementById('visualChartBox');
  var dashboardChartsArea = document.getElementById('dashboardChartsArea');
  var pencapaianChartArea = document.getElementById('pencapaianChartArea');
  var kpiChartArea = document.getElementById('kpiChartArea');
  var summaryPointBox = document.getElementById('summaryPointTableBox');
  var bpSummaryBox = document.getElementById('bpSummaryBox');
  var filterBox = document.getElementById('filterBox');
  var tableBox = document.getElementById('tableBox');
  var dailyBpSummaryBox = document.getElementById('dailyBPSummaryBox');
  var approvalMatrixBox = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixBox');
  var approvalFiltersExtra = document.getElementById('approvalFiltersExtra');
  var monitoringGdToolbarExtra = document.getElementById('monitoringGdToolbarExtra');
  var performaPencapaianBox = document.getElementById('performaPencapaianBox');
  var performaKpWeekBox = document.getElementById('performaKpWeekBox');
  var postDisbMetricsBox = document.getElementById('postDisbMetricsContainer');
  var lastUpdateBadge = document.getElementById('lastUpdateBadge');
  var plafondWarningBanner = document.getElementById('plafondWarningBanner');
  var bahanKunjunganExtraBox = document.getElementById('bahanKunjunganExtraBox');
  var uploadBahanBox = document.getElementById('uploadBahanKunjunganBox');
  var uploadBahanTitle = document.getElementById('uploadBahanKunjunganTitle');
  var statusShortcuts = document.getElementById('statusFilterShortcuts');
  var auditBanner = document.getElementById('auditTrailBanner');

  if (absensiBox) absensiBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (laporanAbsensiBox) laporanAbsensiBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (trackingMapBox) trackingMapBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (dailyBpSummaryBox) dailyBpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (approvalMatrixBox) approvalMatrixBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (performaPencapaianBox) performaPencapaianBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (performaKpWeekBox) performaKpWeekBox.style.display = 'none';
  if (lastUpdateBadge) lastUpdateBadge.style.display = 'none';
  if (plafondWarningBanner) plafondWarningBanner.style.display = 'none';
  
  // Sembunyikan Highlight Card Besar KPI secara default pada menu non-KPI
  if (kpiHeroContainer) kpiHeroContainer.style.display = 'none';

  // ISOLASI KETAT: Kartu metrik Post Disb HANYA diizinkan aktif di menu postDisb saja!
  if (postDisbMetricsBox) postDisbMetricsBox.style.display = (key === 'postDisb') ? 'grid' : 'none';
  if (bahanKunjunganExtraBox) bahanKunjunganExtraBox.style.display = 'none';

  if (dashboardChartsArea) dashboardChartsArea.style.display = 'none';
  if (pencapaianChartArea) pencapaianChartArea.style.display = 'none';
  if (kpiChartArea) kpiChartArea.style.display = 'none';

  var isLoanPaid = ['loanPaidPoint', 'loanPaidArea', 'loanPaidRegional'].indexOf(key) !== -1;
  var isBahanKunjungan = ['cohort7', 'renewal'].indexOf(key) !== -1;
  var isPostDisb = (key === 'postDisb');
  
  // Tampilkan statsBox pada menu Dashboard dan menu aktivitas normal
  if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = (isLoanPaid || isBahanKunjungan || isPostDisb) ? 'none' : 'grid';

  var isLoggedIn = !!window.currentUser;
  if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = isLoggedIn ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = isLoggedIn ? 'block' : 'none';

  var colDateFrom = document.getElementById('filterDateFromCol');
  var colDateTo = document.getElementById('filterDateToCol');
  if (colDateFrom && colDateTo) {
    if (key === 'approvalPerhari' || isLoanPaid) {
      colDateFrom.style.display = 'none';
      colDateTo.style.display = 'none';
    } else {
      colDateFrom.style.display = 'block';
      colDateTo.style.display = 'block';
    }
  }

  var postDisbFiltersExtra = document.getElementById('postDisbFiltersExtra');
  if (approvalFiltersExtra) {
    approvalFiltersExtra.style.display = (key === 'approvalPerhari' && isLoggedIn) ? 'grid' : 'none';
  }
  if (postDisbFiltersExtra) {
    postDisbFiltersExtra.style.display = (key === 'postDisb' && isLoggedIn) ? 'grid' : 'none';
  }
  if (monitoringGdToolbarExtra) {
    monitoringGdToolbarExtra.style.display = (key === 'monitoringGD' && isLoggedIn) ? 'flex' : 'none';
  }

  var pTitle = document.getElementById('pageTitle');
  var pDesc = document.getElementById('pageDescription');
  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';

  // Menampilkan Tabel Summary per Point Spesifik untuk Setiap Menu Aktivitas
  var hasSummaryPointTable = [
    'dashboard', 'sosialisasi', 'pelayanan', 'kunjunganWL', 'kunjunganDO',
    'newPar', 'dpd830', 'dpd3160', 'dpd6190', 'wob', 'validasi', 'suprise', 'renewal'
  ].indexOf(key) !== -1;

  if (summaryPointBox) {
    summaryPointBox.style.display = (hasSummaryPointTable && isLoggedIn) ? 'block' : 'none';
  }

  if (key === 'absensi') {
    if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (absensiBox) absensiBox.style.display = 'block';

    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Absensi Real-time';
    if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Modul presensi kehadiran lapangan berbasis GPS Geofencing & Foto Watermark.';
    if (typeof window.loadAbsensiMasterPoints === 'function') window.loadAbsensiMasterPoints();
    if (typeof window.checkAbsensiUserState === 'function') window.checkAbsensiUserState();
  } else if (key === 'laporanAbsensi') {
    if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (laporanAbsensiBox) laporanAbsensiBox.style.display = 'block';

    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Laporan Summary Absensi';
    if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Rekapitulasi total keikutsertaan dan kehadiran per BP/User.';
    if (typeof window.setupAbsensiFilterRBAC === 'function') window.setupAbsensiFilterRBAC();
    if (typeof window.loadAbsensiSummaryReport === 'function') window.loadAbsensiSummaryReport();
  } else if (key === 'trackingMaps') {
    if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (trackingMapBox) trackingMapBox.style.display = 'block';

    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Live Tracking Lokasi BP (Google Hybrid 24 Jam)';
    if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Memantau pergerakan dan posisi koordinat terakhir BP saat bertugas selama 24 jam via citra satelit Google Hybrid & Street View.';
    if (typeof window.initTrackingMap === 'function') window.initTrackingMap();
  } else if (key === 'performaPencapaian') {
    if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (performaPencapaianBox) performaPencapaianBox.style.display = 'block';

    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Review Performa Pencapaian Regional';
    if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Peringkat Top 5 & Bottom 5 Point Cabang serta Top 10 & Bottom 10 BP berdasarkan % Achievement Total.';
    if (typeof window.loadPerformaPencapaian === 'function') window.loadPerformaPencapaian();
  } else if (key === 'performaKpWeek') {
    if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (performaKpWeekBox) performaKpWeekBox.style.display = 'block';

    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Performa KP Per Week (Day-to-Day Analysis)';
    if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Analisis perbandingan mingguan (W1 s/d W4) dan Day-to-Day (Senin vs Senin, dst.) per Area, Point, dan BP mencakup Sudah Cair, Menunggu, dan Gagal Cair.';
    window.highlightCurrentDayInKpWeek();
    if (typeof window.loadPerformaKpPerWeek === 'function') window.loadPerformaKpPerWeek();
  } else if (key === 'postDisb') {
    if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = isLoggedIn ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'block';
    if (postDisbMetricsBox) postDisbMetricsBox.style.display = 'grid';

    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Konfirmasi Post Disb';
    if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Verifikasi pencairan pembiayaan mitra yang berstatus Sudah Cair via upload bukti sesuai role.';
    if (typeof window.loadPostDisbData === 'function') window.loadPostDisbData();
  } else if (isBahanKunjungan) {
    if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    if (filterBox) filterBox.style.display = isLoggedIn ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (tableBox) tableBox.style.display = 'block';
    if (bahanKunjunganExtraBox) bahanKunjunganExtraBox.style.display = 'block';
    if (statusShortcuts) statusShortcuts.style.display = 'flex';

    var canUpload = (role === '1-RM' || role === '2-AM' || role === 'Editor' || role === 'Admin');

    if (key === 'cohort7') {
      if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'WOB 6+ (Cohort DPD 7+)';
      if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Data mitra DPD 7+ tersinkronisasi status dan dokumentasi foto riil Kunjungan WOB (Cross-Match ID Loan & Fuzzy 90%).';
      if (uploadBahanBox) uploadBahanBox.style.display = 'none';
      if (auditBanner) auditBanner.style.display = 'none';
      if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
    } else if (key === 'renewal') {
      if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = 'Master Leads';
      if (pDesc) pDesc.textContent = 'Data calon mitra (Renewal, Drawdown, Reactivation) tersinkronisasi status kunjungan dan potensi nominal per Point.';
      if (uploadBahanBox) {
        uploadBahanBox.style.display = canUpload ? 'block' : 'none';
        if (uploadBahanTitle) uploadBahanTitle.textContent = 'Unggah Berkas Master Leads (Excel / WPS / CSV)';
      }
      if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'block';
      window.loadBahanKunjunganAuditTrail('renewal');
    }

    window.loadCurrentData();
  } else {
    if (window.trackingInterval) clearInterval(window.trackingInterval);
    var titles = {
      dashboard: 'Dashboard Monitoring',
      monitoringGD: 'Monitoring GD',
      loanPaidPoint: 'Loan Paid Point',
      loanPaidArea: 'Loan Paid Area',
      loanPaidRegional: 'Loan Paid Regional',
      kpiRM: 'Performa KPI RM',
      kpiAM: 'Performa KPI AM',
      kpiBM: 'Performa KPI BM',
      kpi: 'Performa KPI BP',
      pencapaianArea: 'Pencapaian Area',
      pencapaianPoint: 'Pencapaian Point',
      pencapaian: 'Pencapaian BP',
      approvalPerhari: 'KP Daily',
      pelayanan: 'Pelayanan',
      sosialisasi: 'Sosialisasi',
      kunjunganWL: 'Kunjungan Eligible',
      kunjunganDO: 'Kunjungan DO',
      newPar: 'Kunjungan New Par',
      dpd830: 'Kunjungan Mitra DPD 8-30',
      dpd3160: 'Kunjungan Mitra DPD 31-60',
      dpd6190: 'Kunjungan Mitra DPD 61-90',
      wob: 'Kunjungan WOB',
      validasi: 'Validasi UK/Modal BM',
      suprise: 'Suprise Visit'
    };
    if (pTitle) pTitle.textContent = titles[key] || 'Aktivitas';
    if (pDesc) {
      if (key === 'monitoringGD') pDesc.textContent = 'Pemantauan pengajuan Group Loan (GD 1, GD 2, Pending, & Dropout) tersinkronisasi status pencairan KP Daily.';
      else if (isLoanPaid) pDesc.textContent = 'Tabel performa Loan Paid harian & mingguan dengan indikator 2 warna target dan kata Naik/Turun.';
      else pDesc.textContent = 'Data laporan performa & aktivitas dari sheet terkait.';
    }
    
    // PEMULIHAN UTUH TAMPILAN DASHBOARD
    if (key === 'dashboard') {
      if (statsBox) statsBox.style.display = 'grid';
      if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'block';
      if (dashboardChartsArea) dashboardChartsArea.style.display = 'block';
      if (summaryPointBox && isLoggedIn) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'block';
      if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
      if (dailyBpSummaryBox && isLoggedIn) {
        dailyBpSummaryBox.style.display = 'block';
        if (typeof window.loadDailyBPSummary === 'function') window.loadDailyBPSummary();
      }
    } else if (['pencapaianArea', 'pencapaianPoint', 'pencapaian'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
      if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'block';
      if (pencapaianChartArea) pencapaianChartArea.style.display = 'block';
      if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
      if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    } else if (['kpiRM', 'kpiAM', 'kpiBM', 'kpi'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
      if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'block';
      if (kpiChartArea) kpiChartArea.style.display = 'block';
      if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
      if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    } else {
      if (visualChartBox) visualChartBox.style.display = 'none';
      if (bpSummaryBox) bpSummaryBox.style.display = 'none';
    }

    if (key === 'approvalPerhari' && isLoggedIn) {
      if (approvalMatrixBox) approvalMatrixBox.style.display = 'block';
      if (summaryPointBox) summaryPointBox.style.display = 'none';
      if (typeof window.loadApprovalMatrix === 'function') window.loadApprovalMatrix();
    }

    window.loadCurrentData();
  }

  if (window.innerWidth < 760 && typeof window.toggleSidebar === 'function') window.toggleSidebar();
};

window.loadBahanKunjunganAuditTrail = function(menuType) {
  var banner = document.getElementById('auditTrailBanner');
  var uploaderInfo = document.getElementById('auditUploaderInfo');
  var timeBadge = document.getElementById('auditTimestampBadge');
  if (!banner) return;

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getBahanKunjunganAuditTrail === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(audit) {
      if (audit && banner && uploaderInfo && timeBadge) {
        uploaderInfo.textContent = (audit.uploaderRole ? audit.uploaderRole + ' ' : '') + audit.uploaderName + ' (' + audit.fileName + ')';
        timeBadge.textContent = audit.timestampWITA;
        banner.style.display = 'flex';
      } else if (banner) {
        banner.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }).getBahanKunjunganAuditTrail('Master Leads');
  }
};

window.loadCurrentData = function(forceRefresh) {
  var userKey = window.currentUser ? window.currentUser.nik : 'guest';
  var cacheKey = window.currentKey + '_' + userKey;
  var now = Date.now();

  var updateBadgeEl = document.getElementById('lastUpdateBadge');
  var updateTextEl = document.getElementById('lastUpdateText');
  var activeSheetsForUpdate = [
    'pencapaianArea', 'pencapaianPoint', 'pencapaian', 'approvalPerhari',
    'sosialisasi', 'dashboard', 'kpiRM', 'kpiAM', 'kpiBM', 'kpi',
    'monitoringGD', 'loanPaidPoint', 'loanPaidArea', 'loanPaidRegional',
    'cohort7', 'renewal'
  ];

  window.startRefreshTimeTracker();

  if (!forceRefresh && window.dataCache[cacheKey] && (now - window.dataCache[cacheKey].timestamp < window.DATA_CACHE_TTL)) {
    var cached = window.dataCache[cacheKey].data;
    if (cached.restricted) {
      window.handleRestrictedAccess();
      return;
    }
    window.allData = cached.rows || [];
    window.currentHeaders = cached.headers || [];
    window.monitoringGdMetrics = cached.metrics || null;
    window.dashboardMetrics = cached.metrics || null;

    if (activeSheetsForUpdate.indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1 && cached.lastUpdate) {
      if (updateBadgeEl && updateTextEl) {
        updateTextEl.textContent = 'Terakhir Diperbarui: ' + cached.lastUpdate;
        updateBadgeEl.style.display = 'inline-flex';
      }
    } else if (updateBadgeEl) {
      updateBadgeEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    window.populateFilterDropdowns();
    window.applyFilter();
    return;
  }

  var tBody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
  if (tBody) tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="20" class="empty">Memuat data dari Google Sheets...</td></tr>';

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memuat data aktivitas...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run) {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader('100% Selesai');
      if (res.restricted) {
        window.handleRestrictedAccess();
        return;
      }
      window.allData = res.rows || [];
      window.currentHeaders = res.headers || [];
      window.monitoringGdMetrics = res.metrics || null;
      window.dashboardMetrics = res.metrics || null;
      window.dataCache[cacheKey] = { timestamp: Date.now(), data: res };

      if (activeSheetsForUpdate.indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1 && res.lastUpdate) {
        if (updateBadgeEl && updateTextEl) {
          updateTextEl.textContent = 'Terakhir Diperbarui: ' + res.lastUpdate;
          updateBadgeEl.style.display = 'inline-flex';
        }
      } else if (updateBadgeEl) {
        updateBadgeEl.style.display = 'none';
      }

      window.populateFilterDropdowns();
      window.applyFilter();
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.showToast('Gagal memuat data: ' + err.toString());
    }).getData(window.currentKey, window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.handleRestrictedAccess = function() {
  window.allData = [];
  window.filteredData = [];
  var tBody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
  if (tBody) tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="20" class="empty" style="color:#dc2626;">Akses Ditolak! Role Anda tidak memiliki izin untuk melihat data ini.</td></tr>';
  var statsContainer = document.getElementById('stats');
  if (statsContainer) statsContainer.innerHTML = '';
  var box = document.getElementById('visualChartBox');
  if (box) box.style.display = 'none';
};

window.populateFilterDropdowns = function() {
  var bps = {}, points = {}, areas = {}, weeks = {};
  var dataset = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') ? (window.postDisbListRaw || window.postDisbList || []) : window.allData;
  
  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  var uArea = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.area || '').trim().toLowerCase() : '';
  var uPoint = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.point || '').trim().toLowerCase() : '';

  dataset.forEach(function(r) {
    var bp = window.getCellValue(r, 'bpbm') || window.getCellValue(r, 'bp') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || r['_raw_1'] || r['_raw_19'] || r['_raw_12'] || '';
    var pt = window.getCellValue(r, 'point') || window.getCellValue(r, 'branch') || r['_raw_2'] || r['_raw_7'] || '';
    var ar = window.getCellValue(r, 'area') || r['_raw_3'] || r['_raw_6'] || r['_raw_1'] || '';
    var wk = window.getCellValue(r, 'week') || r['_raw_15'] || '';

    if (bp) bps[bp] = true;
    if (pt) points[pt] = true;
    if (ar) areas[ar] = true;
    if (wk) weeks[wk] = true;
  });

  window.masterPointsList.forEach(function(mp) {
    if (role === '2-AM' && uArea && mp.area.toLowerCase() !== uArea) return;
    if ((role === '3-BM' || role === '4-BP' || role === '5-BPR' || role === '6-BP Buffer') && uPoint && mp.point.toLowerCase() !== uPoint) return;
    points[mp.point] = true;
    if (mp.area) areas[mp.area] = true;
  });

  var selBP = document.getElementById('filterBP');
  var selPt = document.getElementById('filterPoint');
  var selAr = document.getElementById('filterArea');
  var selWeek = document.getElementById('gdWeekFilterSelect');

  var curBp = selBP ? selBP.value : '';
  var curPt = selPt ? selPt.value : '';
  var curAr = selAr ? selAr.value : '';
  var curWk = selWeek ? selWeek.value : '';

  if (selBP) {
    selBP.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua BP/BM</option>' + Object.keys(bps).sort().map(function(k) { return '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (curBp) selBP.value = curBp;
  }

  if (selPt) {
    var filteredPointsList = Object.keys(points).filter(function(pName) {
      if (!pName || pName === '-') return false;
      if (role === '2-AM' && uArea) {
        var ptObj = window.masterPointsList.find(function(m) { return m.point.toLowerCase() === pName.toLowerCase(); });
        if (ptObj && ptObj.area.toLowerCase() !== uArea) return false;
      }
      if ((role === '3-BM' || role === '4-BP' || role === '5-BPR' || role === '6-BP Buffer') && uPoint) {
        return pName.toLowerCase() === uPoint;
      }
      return true;
    }).sort();

    selPt.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Point</option>' + filteredPointsList.map(function(k) { return '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (curPt && filteredPointsList.indexOf(curPt) !== -1) selPt.value = curPt;
    else if ((role === '3-BM' || role === '4-BP' || role === '5-BPR') && filteredPointsList.length === 1) {
      selPt.value = filteredPointsList[0];
    }
  }

  if (selAr) {
    var filteredAreasList = Object.keys(areas).filter(function(aName) {
      if (!aName || aName === '-') return false;
      if ((role === '2-AM' || role === '3-BM' || role === '4-BP') && uArea) {
        return aName.toLowerCase() === uArea;
      }
      return true;
    }).sort();

    selAr.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Area</option>' + filteredAreasList.map(function(k) { return '<option value="' + k + '">' + k + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (curAr && filteredAreasList.indexOf(curAr) !== -1) selAr.value = curAr;
    else if (filteredAreasList.length === 1) {
      selAr.value = filteredAreasList[0];
    }
  }

  if (selWeek) {
    var weekList = Object.keys(weeks).sort();
    selWeek.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Week</option>' + weekList.map(function(w) {
      return '<option value="' + w + '">' + w + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    if (curWk && weekList.indexOf(curWk) !== -1) selWeek.value = curWk;
  }
};

window.setGdQuickFilter = function(filterType) {
  window.gdQuickFilterState = filterType || 'all';

  var btnAll = document.getElementById('btnGdFilterAll');
  var btnGD1 = document.getElementById('btnGdFilterGD1');
  var btnGD2 = document.getElementById('btnGdFilterGD2');
  var btnPending = document.getElementById('btnGdFilterPending');
  var btnDropout = document.getElementById('btnGdFilterDropout');

  [btnAll, btnGD1, btnGD2, btnPending, btnDropout].forEach(function(b) {
    if (b) b.className = 'btn-quick-filter-gd';
  });

  if (filterType === 'gd1' && btnGD1) btnGD1.className = 'btn-quick-filter-gd active-gd1';
  else if (filterType === 'gd2' && btnGD2) btnGD2.className = 'btn-quick-filter-gd active-gd2';
  else if (filterType === 'pending' && btnPending) btnPending.className = 'btn-quick-filter-gd active-pending';
  else if (filterType === 'dropout' && btnDropout) btnDropout.className = 'btn-quick-filter-gd active-dropout';
  else if (btnAll) btnAll.className = 'btn-quick-filter-gd active-all';

  window.applyFilter();
};

window.onGdWeekFilterChange = function(weekVal) {
  window.currentGdWeek = String(weekVal || '').trim();
  window.applyFilter();
};

window.setValidationQuickFilter = function(state) {
  window.validationQuickFilterState = state || 'all';
  var btnAll = document.getElementById('btnValAll');
  var btnSudah = document.getElementById('btnValSudah');
  var btnBelum = document.getElementById('btnValBelum');
  var btnGagal = document.getElementById('btnValGagal');

  [btnAll, btnSudah, btnBelum, btnGagal].forEach(function(b) {
    if (b) b.className = 'btn-toggle-val';
  });

  if (state === 'sudah' && btnSudah) btnSudah.className = 'btn-toggle-val active-sudah';
  else if (state === 'belum' && btnBelum) btnBelum.className = 'btn-toggle-val active-belum';
  else if (state === 'gagal' && btnGagal) btnGagal.className = 'btn-toggle-val active-gagal';
  else if (btnAll) btnAll.className = 'btn-toggle-val active-all';

  window.applyFilter();
};

window.toggleDisburseTodayFilter = function() {
  window.isDisburseTodayFilter = !window.isDisburseTodayFilter;
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnDisbToday');
  if (btn) {
    if (window.isDisburseTodayFilter) btn.classList.add('active');
    else btn.classList.remove('active');
  }
  window.applyFilter();
};

window.filterKpWeekDay = function(dayName) {
  window.currentKpWeekDay = dayName || 'all';
  document.querySelectorAll('.kp-week-day-btn').forEach(function(btn) {
    if (btn.getAttribute('data-day') === window.currentKpWeekDay) {
      btn.classList.add('active');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('active');
    }
  });

  if (typeof window.renderKpWeekTable === 'function') {
    window.renderKpWeekTable();
  }
};

window.applyFilter = function() {
  var isLoanPaid = ['loanPaidPoint', 'loanPaidArea', 'loanPaidRegional'].indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1;
  var isBahanKunjungan = ['cohort7', 'renewal'].indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1;

  var q = document.getElementById('search') ? document.getElementById('search').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var bp = document.getElementById('filterBP') ? document.getElementById('filterBP').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var pt = document.getElementById('filterPoint') ? document.getElementById('filterPoint').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var ar = document.getElementById('filterArea') ? document.getElementById('filterArea').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  
  var df = document.getElementById('dateFrom') ? document.getElementById('dateFrom').value : '';
  var dt = document.getElementById('dateTo') ? document.getElementById('dateTo').value : '';

  // Single Date filter logic
  var singleDateBadgeWrapper = document.getElementById('singleDateBadgeWrapper');
  var singleDateFilterText = document.getElementById('singleDateFilterText');
  var isSingleDate = (df && !dt);

  if (singleDateBadgeWrapper && singleDateFilterText) {
    if (isSingleDate) {
      var dParts = df.split('-');
      var formattedDateStr = (dParts.length === 3) ? (dParts[2] + '/' + dParts[1] + '/' + dParts[0]) : df;
      singleDateFilterText.textContent = formattedDateStr;
      singleDateBadgeWrapper.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
      singleDateBadgeWrapper.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  var filterProd = document.getElementById('filterProductType') ? document.getElementById('filterProductType').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var filterCyc = document.getElementById('filterKategoriCycle') ? document.getElementById('filterKategoriCycle').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  
  var filterPostSt = document.getElementById('filterPostDisbStatus') ? document.getElementById('filterPostDisbStatus').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';
  var filterPostPr = document.getElementById('filterPostDisbProduct') ? document.getElementById('filterPostDisbProduct').value.toLowerCase().trim() : '';

  var dfApprove = document.getElementById('dateFromApprove') ? document.getElementById('dateFromApprove').value : '';
  var dtApprove = document.getElementById('dateToApprove') ? document.getElementById('dateToApprove').value : '';
  var dfDisb = document.getElementById('dateFromDisb') ? document.getElementById('dateFromDisb').value : '';
  var dtDisb = document.getElementById('dateToDisb') ? document.getElementById('dateToDisb').value : '';

  var dfObj = df ? window.parseDateStr(df) : null;
  var dtObj = dt ? window.parseDateStr(dt) : null;
  if (dfObj) dfObj.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  if (dtObj) dtObj.setHours(23,59,59,999);

  var dfApproveObj = dfApprove ? window.parseDateStr(dfApprove) : null;
  var dtApproveObj = dtApprove ? window.parseDateStr(dtApprove) : null;
  if (dfApproveObj) dfApproveObj.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  if (dtApproveObj) dtApproveObj.setHours(23,59,59,999);

  var dfDisbObj = dfDisb ? window.parseDateStr(dfDisb) : null;
  var dtDisbObj = dtDisb ? window.parseDateStr(dtDisb) : null;
  if (dfDisbObj) dfDisbObj.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  if (dtDisbObj) dtDisbObj.setHours(23,59,59,999);

  window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly = false;
  var btnDup = document.getElementById('btnFilterDuplicate');
  if (btnDup) {
    btnDup.textContent = 'Lihat Data Duplikat';
    btnDup.style.background = '#ea580c';
  }

  window.lowPlafondList = [];
  var countLowPlafondTotal = 0;

  var sourceData = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') ? (window.postDisbListRaw || window.postDisbList) : window.allData;

  // Real-time counter for Status Validasi Cepat in KP Daily
  var cntAll = 0, cntSudah = 0, cntBelum = 0, cntGagal = 0;

  window.filteredData = sourceData.filter(function(r) {
    var rowBp = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'bpbm') || window.getCellValue(r, 'bp') || window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || r['_raw_1'] || r['_raw_19'] || r['_raw_12'] || '').toLowerCase();
    var rowPt = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'point') || window.getCellValue(r, 'branch') || r['_raw_2'] || r['_raw_7'] || '').toLowerCase();
    var rowAr = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'area') || r['_raw_3'] || r['_raw_6'] || r['_raw_1'] || '').toLowerCase();
    
    var rowDateStr = (window.currentKey === 'dashboard') ?
      (window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal') || r['_raw_12'] || window.getCellValue(r, 'submittedat') || r['_raw_0'] || '') :
      (window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal_approve_uk') || window.getCellValue(r, 'disbursement_date') || window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal') || window.getCellValue(r, 'submittedat') || r['_raw_0'] || '');

    if (bp && rowBp.indexOf(bp) === -1) return false;
    if (pt && rowPt.indexOf(pt) === -1) return false;
    if (ar && rowAr !== ar) return false;

    // Filter Khusus Monitoring GD
    if (window.currentKey === 'monitoringGD') {
      var rWeek = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'week') || r['_raw_15'] || '').trim();
      var rGdType = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'gd_2') || r['_raw_14'] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      var rStatusGagal = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'status_gagal_dropping') || r['_raw_13'] || '').trim().toUpperCase();

      if (window.currentGdWeek && rWeek !== window.currentGdWeek) return false;

      if (window.gdQuickFilterState === 'gd1') {
        if (rGdType.indexOf('GD 1') === -1 && rGdType !== '1' && rGdType !== 'GD1') return false;
      } else if (window.gdQuickFilterState === 'gd2') {
        if (rGdType.indexOf('GD 2') === -1 && rGdType !== '2' && rGdType !== 'GD2') return false;
      } else if (window.gdQuickFilterState === 'pending') {
        if (rStatusGagal.indexOf('PENDING') === -1) return false;
      } else if (window.gdQuickFilterState === 'dropout') {
        if (rStatusGagal.indexOf('DROPOUT') === -1) return false;
      }
    }

    // Filter Khusus Bahan Kunjungan
    if (isBahanKunjungan && window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus && window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus !== 'all') {
      var isVisited = !!r.is_visited || (String(window.getCellValue(r, 'status_kunjungan')).toLowerCase().indexOf('sudah') !== -1);
      var stMinat = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'status_minat')).toLowerCase();

      if (window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus === 'visited' && !isVisited) return false;
      if (window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus === 'unvisited' && isVisited) return false;
      if (window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus === 'minat' && stMinat.indexOf('minat') === -1) return false;
      if (window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus === 'tidak_minat' && stMinat.indexOf('tidak') === -1) return false;
    }

    if (window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari') {
      var rProd = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'product_type') || '').toLowerCase();
      var rCyc = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'kategori_cycle') || '').toLowerCase();
      var rValStatus = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'status_validasi') || '').trim();
      var rStatusCair = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'status_cair') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var disbVal = window.getCellValue(r, 'disbursement_date');

      if (window.isDisburseTodayFilter) {
        if (!window.isToday(disbVal)) return false;
      }

      if (filterProd) {
        if (filterProd === 'modal' && rProd.indexOf('modal') === -1) return false;
        if (filterProd === 'group loan' && rProd.indexOf('grub') === -1 && rProd.indexOf('group') === -1) return false;
      }

      if (filterCyc) {
        if (filterCyc === 'new' && (rCyc.indexOf('new') === -1 && rCyc.indexOf('baru') === -1)) return false;
        if (filterCyc === 'subsequent' && (rCyc.indexOf('subsequent') === -1 && rCyc.indexOf('lanjutan') === -1)) return false;
      }

      if (dfApproveObj || dtApproveObj) {
        var appDateStr = window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal_approve_uk');
        var appDate = window.parseDateStr(appDateStr);
        if (appDate) {
          appDate.setHours(12,0,0,0);
          if (dfApproveObj && dtApproveObj) {
            if (appDate < dfApproveObj || appDate > dtApproveObj) return false;
          } else if (dfApproveObj) {
            if (appDate < dfApproveObj) return false;
          } else if (dtApproveObj) {
            if (appDate > dtApproveObj) return false;
          }
        } else return false;
      }

      if (dfDisbObj || dtDisbObj) {
        var disbDateStr = window.getCellValue(r, 'disbursement_date');
        var disbDate = window.parseDateStr(disbDateStr);
        if (disbDate) {
          disbDate.setHours(12,0,0,0);
          if (dfDisbObj && dtDisbObj) {
            if (disbDate < dfDisbObj || disbDate > dtDisbObj) return false;
          } else if (dfDisbObj) {
            if (disbDate < dfDisbObj) return false;
          } else if (dtDisbObj) {
            if (disbDate > dtDisbObj) return false;
          }
        } else return false;
      }

      var isGagalCair = (rStatusCair.indexOf('gagal') !== -1);
      var isMenungguAtauCair = (rStatusCair.indexOf('menunggu') !== -1 || rStatusCair.indexOf('sudah cair') !== -1 || rStatusCair === 'cair');

      // Update counters based on area/point/date filters
      cntAll++;
      if (isGagalCair) {
        cntGagal++;
      } else if (rValStatus === 'Sudah Validasi') {
        cntSudah++;
      } else if (rValStatus === 'Belum Validasi' && isMenungguAtauCair) {
        cntBelum++;
      }

      // Apply the selected quick filter state
      if (window.validationQuickFilterState === 'sudah' && rValStatus !== 'Sudah Validasi') return false;
      if (window.validationQuickFilterState === 'belum') {
        if (rValStatus !== 'Belum Validasi' || !isMenungguAtauCair) return false;
      }
      if (window.validationQuickFilterState === 'gagal' && !isGagalCair) return false;

      // Check Plafond <= 4jt alert
      var plfNum = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'plafond') || r['_raw_7']);
      var isMenungguCair = (rStatusCair.indexOf('menunggu') !== -1);
      if (isMenungguCair && plfNum > 0 && plfNum <= 4000000) {
        countLowPlafondTotal++;
        if (plfNum < 3000000) {
          window.lowPlafondList.push(r);
        }
      }
    }

    if (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') {
      var rPostSt = String(r.status_post_disb || window.getCellValue(r, 'status_post_disb') || '').toLowerCase();
      var rPostPr = String(r.product_type || window.getCellValue(r, 'product_type') || '').toLowerCase();
      
      if (filterPostSt) {
        if (filterPostSt === 'sudah' && rPostSt.indexOf('sudah') === -1) return false;
        if (filterPostSt === 'belum' && rPostSt.indexOf('belum') === -1) return false;
        if (filterPostSt === 'tidak direspon' && rPostSt.indexOf('tidak direspon') === -1) return false;
        if (filterPostSt === 'tidak bisa dihubungi' && rPostSt.indexOf('tidak bisa dihubungi') === -1) return false;
      }
      if (filterPostPr) {
        if (filterPostPr === 'modal' && rPostPr.indexOf('modal') === -1) return false;
        if (filterPostPr === 'group loan' && rPostPr.indexOf('grub') === -1 && rPostPr.indexOf('group') === -1) return false;
      }
    }

    // Universal Date Filter
    if (window.currentKey !== 'approvalPerhari' && !isLoanPaid && (dfObj || dtObj)) {
      var isDateSheet = ['dashboard', 'postDisb', 'pelayanan', 'sosialisasi', 'kunjunganWL', 'kunjunganDO', 'newPar', 'dpd830', 'dpd3160', 'dpd6190', 'wob', 'validasi', 'suprise', 'monitoringGD', 'cohort7', 'renewal'].indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1;
      if (isDateSheet) {
        var rowD = window.parseDateStr(rowDateStr);
        if (rowD) {
          if (isSingleDate && dfObj) {
            if (rowD.getDate() !== dfObj.getDate() || rowD.getMonth() !== dfObj.getMonth() || rowD.getFullYear() !== dfObj.getFullYear()) {
              return false;
            }
          } else {
            rowD.setHours(12,0,0,0);
            if (dfObj && dtObj) {
              if (rowD < dfObj || rowD > dtObj) return false;
            } else if (dfObj) {
              if (rowD < dfObj) return false;
            } else if (dtObj) {
              if (rowD > dtObj) return false;
            }
          }
        } else {
          return false;
        }
      }
    }

    if (q) {
      var fullStr = JSON.stringify(r).toLowerCase();
      if (fullStr.indexOf(q) === -1) return false;
    }

    return true;
  });

  // Update dynamic counter badges for Status Validasi Cepat in KP Daily
  if (window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari') {
    var elAll = document.getElementById('valAllCount');
    var elSudah = document.getElementById('valSudahCount');
    var elBelum = document.getElementById('valBelumCount');
    var elGagal = document.getElementById('valGagalCount');
    if (elAll) elAll.textContent = cntAll;
    if (elSudah) elSudah.textContent = cntSudah;
    if (elBelum) elBelum.textContent = cntBelum;
    if (elGagal) elGagal.textContent = cntGagal;
  }

  // Tampilkan banner warning Plafond <= 4jt
  var warnBanner = document.getElementById('plafondWarningBanner');
  var warnTitle = document.getElementById('plafondWarningTitleText');
  var warnSub = document.getElementById('plafondWarningSubText');
  if (warnBanner) {
    if (window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari' && countLowPlafondTotal > 0) {
      warnBanner.style.display = 'flex';
      if (warnTitle) warnTitle.textContent = 'Peringatan: Terdeteksi ' + countLowPlafondTotal + ' Pengajuan Plafond ≤ Rp 4.000.000 (Menunggu Pencairan)';
      if (warnSub) warnSub.textContent = 'Terdapat ' + window.lowPlafondList.length + ' pengajuan plafond < Rp 3.000.000. BM dapat membuka rincian untuk evaluasi reject ke AM.';
    } else {
      warnBanner.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  if (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') {
    window.postDisbList = window.filteredData;
  }

  // Sort rows descending by date
  if (!isLoanPaid) {
    window.filteredData.sort(function(a, b) {
      var dateAStr = (window.currentKey === 'dashboard') ?
        (window.getCellValue(a, 'tanggal') || a['_raw_12'] || a['_raw_0'] || '') :
        (window.getCellValue(a, 'tanggal_approve_uk') || window.getCellValue(a, 'disbursement_date') || window.getCellValue(a, 'tanggal') || a['_raw_0'] || '');
      
      var dateBStr = (window.currentKey === 'dashboard') ?
        (window.getCellValue(b, 'tanggal') || b['_raw_12'] || b['_raw_0'] || '') :
        (window.getCellValue(b, 'tanggal_approve_uk') || window.getCellValue(b, 'disbursement_date') || window.getCellValue(b, 'tanggal') || b['_raw_0'] || '');
      
      var dateA = window.parseDateStr(dateAStr);
      var dateB = window.parseDateStr(dateBStr);
      
      var timeA = (dateA && !isNaN(dateA.getTime())) ? dateA.getTime() : 0;
      var timeB = (dateB && !isNaN(dateB.getTime())) ? dateB.getTime() : 0;
      
      return timeB - timeA;
    });
  }

  window.currentPage = 1;
  window.renderMetricCards();
  if (typeof window.renderCharts === 'function') window.renderCharts();
  
  if (typeof window.renderPointSummaryTable === 'function') {
    window.renderPointSummaryTable(window.currentKey, window.filteredData);
  }

  if (typeof window.renderTable === 'function') window.renderTable();

  if (window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari' && window.currentUser && typeof window.loadApprovalMatrix === 'function') {
    window.loadApprovalMatrix(false, window.currentMatrixWeekIdx, window.currentMatrixMonthKey);
  }

  if (window.currentKey === 'dashboard' && window.currentUser && typeof window.loadDailyBPSummary === 'function') {
    window.loadDailyBPSummary();
  }
};

window.resetFilter = function() {
  if (document.getElementById('search')) document.getElementById('search').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('filterBP')) document.getElementById('filterBP').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('filterPoint')) document.getElementById('filterPoint').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('filterArea')) document.getElementById('filterArea').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('dateFrom')) document.getElementById('dateFrom').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('dateTo')) document.getElementById('dateTo').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('filterProductType')) document.getElementById('filterProductType').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('filterKategoriCycle')) document.getElementById('filterKategoriCycle').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('dateFromApprove')) document.getElementById('dateFromApprove').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('dateToApprove')) document.getElementById('dateToApprove').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('dateFromDisb')) document.getElementById('dateFromDisb').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('dateToDisb')) document.getElementById('dateToDisb').value = '';
  if (document.getElementById('gdWeekFilterSelect')) document.getElementById('gdWeekFilterSelect').value = '';
  
  window.isDisburseTodayFilter = false;
  var btnDisbToday = document.getElementById('btnDisbToday');
  if (btnDisbToday) btnDisbToday.classList.remove('active');

  var singleDateBadgeWrapper = document.getElementById('singleDateBadgeWrapper');
  if (singleDateBadgeWrapper) singleDateBadgeWrapper.style.display = 'none';

  var badgePointWrapper = document.getElementById('activePointFilterBadgeWrapper');
  if (badgePointWrapper) badgePointWrapper.style.display = 'none';

  window.currentBahanKunjunganStatus = 'all';
  document.querySelectorAll('.btn-shortcut-status').forEach(function(b) {
    if (b.getAttribute('data-status') === 'all') b.classList.add('active');
    else b.classList.remove('active');
  });

  window.setValidationQuickFilter('all');
  window.setGdQuickFilter('all');
  window.currentGdWeek = '';
  window.populateFilterDropdowns();
  window.applyFilter();
};

window.changePageSize = function(val) {
  window.pageSize = (val === 'all') ? 'all' : parseInt(val, 10);
  window.currentPage = 1;
  if (typeof window.renderTable === 'function') window.renderTable();
};

window.exportToCSV = function() {
  var dataset = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') ? window.postDisbList : window.filteredData;
  if (!dataset || dataset.length === 0) {
    window.showToast('Tidak ada data untuk diekspor!');
    return;
  }
  
  var cols = (typeof window.getDisplayColumns === 'function') ? window.getDisplayColumns(window.currentKey, false) : [];
  var csvContent = "\uFEFF";
  var headers = cols.map(function(c) { return '"' + c.label + '"'; }).join(",");
  csvContent += headers + "\r\n";
  
  dataset.forEach(function(row) {
    var rowArray = cols.map(function(col) {
      var val = window.getCellValue(row, col.key, col.rawIdx) || '-';
      return '"' + String(val).replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
    });
    csvContent += rowArray.join(",") + "\r\n";
  });
  
  var blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  var link = document.createElement("a");
  link.setAttribute("href", url);
  link.setAttribute("download", "Export_Data_" + window.currentKey + "_" + Date.now() + ".csv");
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  setTimeout(function() { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); }, 200);
};

window.openRejectPlafondModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('rejectPlafondModal');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('rejectPlafondTableBody');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('rejectPlafondCount');
  if (!modal || !tBody) return;

  var list = window.lowPlafondList || [];
  if (cnt) cnt.textContent = list.length + ' mitra terdeteksi pengajuan < Rp 3.000.000';

  if (list.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="7" class="empty">Tidak ada data mitra dengan plafond < Rp 3.000.000.</td></tr>';
  } else {
    tBody.innerHTML = list.map(function(r) {
