      { key: 'status_kunjungan', label: 'STATUS KUNJUNGAN' },
      { key: 'status_minat', label: 'STATUS MINAT' },
      { key: 'bukti_foto', label: 'BUKTI FOTO ELIGIBLE' },
      { key: 'agent_fullname', label: 'BP MAJELIS' },
      { key: 'customer_number', label: 'CUSTOMER NUMBER' },
      { key: 'customer_name', label: 'CUSTOMER NAME' },
      { key: 'campaign', label: 'CAMPAIGN' },
      { key: 'flagging_mitra', label: 'FLAGGING MITRA' },
      { key: 'regional', label: 'REGIONAL' },
      { key: 'area', label: 'AREA' },
      { key: 'branch', label: 'BRANCH' },
      { key: 'loan_id', label: 'LOAN ID' },
      { key: 'plafond', label: 'PLAFOND' }
    ];
  }

  if (colConfigs[key]) return colConfigs[key];

  if (window.currentHeaders && window.currentHeaders.length > 0) {
    return window.currentHeaders.map(function(h, idx) {
      return { key: h.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''), label: h, rawIdx: idx };
    });
  }

  return [];
};

window.renderTable = function() {
  var tHead = document.getElementById('tableHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
  var rowCountEl = document.getElementById('rowCount');
  var pagInfo = document.getElementById('pagInfo');
  var pagControls = document.getElementById('pagControls');
  var dupBanner = document.getElementById('duplicateBanner');
  var dupText = document.getElementById('duplicateBannerText');

  if (!tHead || !tBody) return;

  var isLoanPaid = ['loanPaidPoint', 'loanPaidArea', 'loanPaidRegional'].indexOf(window.currentKey) !== -1;
  var isPostDisb = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb');
  var dataset = isPostDisb ? (window.postDisbList || []) : (window.filteredData || []);

  window.visitIndexMap = {};
  window.mitraTotalVisitsMap = {};
  var countDuplicates = 0;

  if (window.currentKey === 'kunjunganWL' || window.currentKey === 'kunjunganDO') {
    var trackerMap = {};

    dataset.forEach(function(r) {
      var rawId = window.getCellValue(r, 'idloan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'id_loan') || window.getCellValue(r, 'loan_id');
      var cleanId = window.normalizeNumericId(rawId);
      var namaMitra = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'namamitra') || window.getCellValue(r, 'customer_name') || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      var mKey = '';

      if (cleanId && cleanId !== '0') {
        mKey = 'ID_' + cleanId;
      } else if (namaMitra) {
        var foundKey = null;
        for (var k in trackerMap) {
          if (k.indexOf('NM_') === 0) {
            var exName = k.substring(3);
            if (window.calculateNameSimilarity(exName, namaMitra) >= 0.90) {
              foundKey = k;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        mKey = foundKey || ('NM_' + namaMitra.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''));
      }

      if (mKey) {
        if (!trackerMap[mKey]) trackerMap[mKey] = [];
        trackerMap[mKey].push(r);
      }
    });

    Object.keys(trackerMap).forEach(function(mk) {
      var arr = trackerMap[mk];
      window.mitraTotalVisitsMap[mk] = arr.length;
      if (arr.length > 1) countDuplicates++;
      arr.forEach(function(rItem, idx) {
        rItem['_visit_seq_num'] = idx + 1;
        rItem['_mitra_key'] = mk;
        rItem['_total_visits'] = arr.length;
      });
    });

    if (dupBanner && dupText) {
      if (countDuplicates > 0) {
        dupBanner.style.display = 'flex';
        dupText.textContent = 'Terdeteksi ' + countDuplicates + ' mitra yang dikunjungi lebih dari 1 kali (Visit Berulang).';
      } else {
        dupBanner.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }

    if (window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly) {
      dataset = dataset.filter(function(r) {
        return r['_total_visits'] > 1;
      });
    }
  } else if (dupBanner) {
    dupBanner.style.display = 'none';
  }

  var totalRows = dataset.length;
  if (rowCountEl) rowCountEl.textContent = totalRows.toLocaleString('id-ID') + ' data';

  if (totalRows === 0) {
    tHead.innerHTML = '';
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="20" class="empty">Tidak ada data yang sesuai filter.</td></tr>';
    if (pagInfo) pagInfo.textContent = 'Menampilkan 0 data';
    if (pagControls) pagControls.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  if (isLoanPaid) {
    window.renderLoanPaidTable(tHead, tBody, dataset);
    if (pagInfo) pagInfo.textContent = 'Menampilkan seluruh ' + totalRows + ' baris Loan Paid';
    if (pagControls) pagControls.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  var pSize = window.pageSize === 'all' ? totalRows : window.pageSize;
  var totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalRows / pSize));
  if (window.currentPage > totalPages) window.currentPage = totalPages;

  var startIdx = (window.currentPage - 1) * pSize;
  var endIdx = Math.min(startIdx + pSize, totalRows);
  var pageRows = dataset.slice(startIdx, endIdx);
  var cols = window.getDisplayColumns(window.currentKey, isPostDisb);

  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' + cols.map(function(c) {
    return '<th>' + c.label + '</th>';
  }).join('') + '</tr>';

  tBody.innerHTML = pageRows.map(function(row, rIdx) {
    var globalRowIndex = startIdx + rIdx;
    var rowClass = '';
    if (window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari') {
      var plfNum = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(row, 'plafond') || row['_raw_7']);
      var stCair = String(window.getCellValue(row, 'status_cair') || '').toLowerCase();
      if (stCair.indexOf('menunggu') !== -1 && plfNum > 0 && plfNum <= 4000000) {
        rowClass = 'class="tr-plafond-warning"';
      }
    }

    return '<tr ' + rowClass + '>' + cols.map(function(col) {
      return '<td>' + window.renderCellContent(row, col, globalRowIndex) + '</td>';
    }).join('') + '</tr>';
  }).join('');

  if (pagInfo) pagInfo.textContent = 'Menampilkan ' + (startIdx + 1) + ' - ' + endIdx + ' dari ' + totalRows + ' data';

  if (pagControls) {
    var ctrlHtml = '';
    if (totalPages > 1) {
      ctrlHtml += '<button type="button" class="pag-btn" onclick="window.changePage(' + (window.currentPage - 1) + ')" ' + (window.currentPage === 1 ? 'disabled' : '') + '>‹ Prev</button>';
      var startP = Math.max(1, window.currentPage - 2);
      var endP = Math.min(totalPages, startP + 4);
      for (var p = startP; p <= endP; p++) {
        ctrlHtml += '<button type="button" class="pag-btn ' + (p === window.currentPage ? 'active' : '') + '" onclick="window.changePage(' + p + ')">' + p + '</button>';
      }
      ctrlHtml += '<button type="button" class="pag-btn" onclick="window.changePage(' + (window.currentPage + 1) + ')" ' + (window.currentPage === totalPages ? 'disabled' : '') + '>Next ›</button>';
    }
    pagControls.innerHTML = ctrlHtml;
  }
};

window.changePage = function(p) {
  var dataset = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') ? (window.postDisbList || []) : (window.filteredData || []);
  var pSize = window.pageSize === 'all' ? dataset.length : window.pageSize;
  var totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dataset.length / pSize));

  if (p < 1) p = 1;
  if (p > totalPages) p = totalPages;

  window.currentPage = p;
  window.renderTable();

  var tblBox = document.getElementById('tableBox');
  if (tblBox) {
    tblBox.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  }
};

window.toggleDuplicateFilter = function() {
  window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly = !window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly;
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnFilterDuplicate');
  if (btn) {
    if (window.isShowingDuplicatesOnly) {
      btn.textContent = '↺ Tampilkan Semua Data';
      btn.style.background = '#0f172a';
    } else {
      btn.textContent = 'Lihat Data Duplikat';
      btn.style.background = '#ea580c';
    }
  }
  window.currentPage = 1;
  window.renderTable();
};

window.renderCellContent = function(row, col, rowIndex) {
  var val = window.getCellValue(row, col.key, col.rawIdx);

  if (col.key === 'actions' && window.currentKey === 'postDisb') {
    var st = String(row.status_post_disb || '').trim();
    var isVer = (st === 'Sudah Post Disb');
    return '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;">' +
      (isVer ?
        '<button type="button" class="auth-btn logout-btn" style="padding:4px 9px;font-size:10px;" onclick="window.openUnverifyPostDisbModal(' + rowIndex + ')">Batal</button>' :
        '<button type="button" class="auth-btn" style="padding:4px 9px;font-size:10px;background:#16a34a;" onclick="window.openPostDisbVerifyModal(' + rowIndex + ')">Verifikasi</button>'
      ) +
      '<button type="button" class="auth-btn" style="padding:4px 8px;font-size:10px;background:#0284c7;" onclick="window.openContactMitraModal(' + rowIndex + ')">📞 Hubungi</button>' +
    '</div>';
  }

  if (col.key === 'aksi' && window.currentKey === 'dashboard') {
    var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
    var canDelete = (role === '1-RM' || role === '2-AM' || role === 'Editor' || role === 'Admin');
    if (canDelete) {
      return '<button type="button" class="action-delete-btn" onclick="window.confirmDeleteHasilInputan(' + rowIndex + ')">Hapus</button>';
    }
    return '-';
  }

  if (col.key === 'point' || col.key === 'branch') {
    var ptResolved = window.getCellValue(row, 'point') || window.getCellValue(row, 'branch') || row['_raw_2'] || row['_raw_1'] || row['_raw_7'] || '';
    if (ptResolved && ptResolved !== '-') return '<strong>' + ptResolved + '</strong>';
    return '-';
  }

  if (col.key === 'bp' || col.key === 'bpbm') {
    var bpResolved = window.getCellValue(row, 'bp') || window.getCellValue(row, 'bpbm') || window.getCellValue(row, 'nama') || row['_raw_13'] || row['_raw_1'] || '';
    if (bpResolved && bpResolved !== '-') return bpResolved;
    return '-';
  }

  if (col.key === 'status_validasi' && window.currentKey === 'approvalPerhari') {
    var sVal = String(row.status_validasi || val || '').trim();
    var isSudah = (sVal === 'Sudah Validasi');
    var stCair = String(window.getCellValue(row, 'status_cair') || '').toLowerCase();
    var isGagalCair = (stCair.indexOf('gagal') !== -1);

    if (isGagalCair) {
      return '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#f1f5f9;color:#64748b;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;">Tidak Perlu Validasi</span>';
    }

    var badge = isSudah ?
      '<span class="badge-pill success">Sudah Validasi</span>' :
      '<span class="badge-pill danger">Belum Validasi</span>';

    if (isSudah && row.foto_validasi) {
      badge += ' <button type="button" class="table-photo-btn" style="padding:2px 6px;font-size:9.5px;margin-left:4px;" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + row.foto_validasi + '\', \'Bukti Foto Validasi BM\')">📷 Foto</button>';
    }
    return badge;
  }

  if (col.key === 'status_post_disb') {
    var stP = String(val || '').trim();
    if (stP === 'Sudah Post Disb') return '<span class="badge-pill success">Sudah Post Disb</span>';
    if (stP === 'Tidak Direspon') return '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#fef3c7;color:#b45309;border:1px solid #fde68a;">Tidak Direspon</span>';
    if (stP === 'Tidak Bisa Dihubungi') return '<span class="badge-pill danger">Tidak Bisa Dihubungi</span>';
    return '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#f1f5f9;color:#64748b;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;">Belum Post Disb</span>';
  }

  if (col.key === 'status_kunjungan') {
    var stK = String(val || '').trim();
    var isVis = (stK === 'Sudah Dikunjungi' || row.is_visited);
    var matchBadge = row.matching_type === 'id_match' ? ' <small style="background:#dbeafe;color:#1e40af;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px;">ID Match</small>' :
                     row.matching_type === 'name_match' ? ' <small style="background:#fef3c7;color:#92400e;padding:1px 4px;border-radius:3px;">Fuzzy 90%</small>' : '';
    if (isVis) return '<span class="badge-pill success">Sudah Dikunjungi</span>' + matchBadge;
    return '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#f1f5f9;color:#64748b;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;">Belum Dikunjungi</span>';
  }

  if (col.key === 'status_minat') {
    var stM = String(val || '').toLowerCase();
    if (stM.indexOf('minat') !== -1 && stM.indexOf('tidak') === -1 && stM.indexOf('belum') === -1) {
      return '<span class="badge-pill success">Minat</span>';
    } else if (stM.indexOf('tidak') !== -1) {
      return '<span class="badge-pill danger">Tidak Minat</span>';
    }
    return val || '-';
  }

  if (col.key === 'dokumentasifoto' || col.key === 'bukti_url' || col.key === 'bukti_foto_kunjungan' || col.key === 'bukti_foto' || col.key === 'bukti_post_disb') {
    var photoUrl = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(val);
    if (photoUrl && photoUrl !== '-') {
      return '<button type="button" class="table-photo-btn" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + photoUrl + '\', \'Dokumentasi Lapangan\')">📷 Buka Foto</button>';
    }
    return '-';
  }

  if (col.key === 'hasilvisit' || col.key === 'hasil_kunjungan') {
    var strHasil = String(val || '-');
    if (strHasil.length > 55) {
      return '<div class="hasil-visit-cell">' +
        '<div class="hasil-visit-truncate">' + strHasil + '</div>' +
        '<button type="button" class="visit-more-link" onclick="window.openVisitDetailModal(' + rowIndex + ')">Lihat Selengkapnya ↗</button>' +
      '</div>';
    }
    return strHasil;
  }

  if (col.key === 'customer_name' || col.key === 'namamitra' || col.key === 'borrower') {
    var cName = String(val || '-');
    var phone = window.getCellValue(row, 'phone_no') || window.getCellValue(row, 'nohp');
    var callIcon = phone ? ' <button type="button" style="border:none;background:transparent;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;" onclick="window.openContactMitraModal(' + rowIndex + ')" title="Hubungi Mitra">📞</button>' : '';
    var dupBadge = '';
    if (row['_total_visits'] > 1) {
      dupBadge = '<span class="badge-visit-count" title="Mitra dikunjungi ' + row['_total_visits'] + ' kali">Visit ke-' + (row['_visit_seq_num'] || 1) + '</span>';
    }
    return '<strong>' + cName + '</strong>' + callIcon + dupBadge;
  }

  var colLabelLower = String(col.label || '').toLowerCase();
  var colKeyLower = String(col.key || '').toLowerCase();
  var isCurrencyCol = colKeyLower === 'plafond' || colKeyLower === 'nominalcollect' || colKeyLower === 'principal_amount' || 
                      colKeyLower === 'totalplafond' || colKeyLower === 'totaldisb' || colKeyLower === 'outstanding_amount' ||
                      colLabelLower.indexOf('plafond') !== -1 || colLabelLower.indexOf('amount') !== -1 || 
                      colLabelLower.indexOf('nominal') !== -1 || colLabelLower.indexOf('potensi') !== -1;

  if (isCurrencyCol) {
    var num = window.numVal(val);
    if (num > 0) return 'Rp ' + num.toLocaleString('id-ID');
    return val || '-';
  }

  return val || '-';
};

window.renderLoanPaidTable = function(tHead, tBody, rows) {
  var headers = window.currentHeaders || [];
  if (!headers || headers.length === 0) return;

  var transformedHeaders = headers.map(function(h) {
    var clean = String(h || '').trim();
    var lower = clean.toLowerCase();

    // Transformasi Header Daily / Aktual
    if ((lower.indexOf('dayli') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('daily') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('aktual') !== -1) && (lower.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('dpd0') !== -1)) {
      return 'DAYLI DPD 0 (98%)';
    }
    if ((lower.indexOf('dayli') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('daily') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('aktual') !== -1) && (lower.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('dpd 1-30') !== -1)) {
      return 'DAYLI 1-30 (55%)';
    }
    if ((lower.indexOf('dayli') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('daily') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('aktual') !== -1) && (lower.indexOf('31-90') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('dpd 31-90') !== -1)) {
      return 'DAYLI 31-90 (13%)';
    }

    // Transformasi Header W1/W2/W3/Week Sebelumnya -> WEEK SEBELUMNYA DPD...
    if ((lower.indexOf('week sebelumnya') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w1') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w2') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w3') !== -1) && (lower.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('dpd0') !== -1)) {
      return 'WEEK SEBELUMNYA DPD 0 (98%)';
    }
    if ((lower.indexOf('week sebelumnya') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w1') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w2') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w3') !== -1) && (lower.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('dpd 1-30') !== -1)) {
      return 'WEEK SEBELUMNYA 1-30 (55%)';
    }
    if ((lower.indexOf('week sebelumnya') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w1') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w2') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('w3') !== -1) && (lower.indexOf('31-90') !== -1 || lower.indexOf('dpd 31-90') !== -1)) {
      return 'WEEK SEBELUMNYA 31-90 (13%)';
    }

    return clean;
  });

  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' +
    transformedHeaders.map(function(h) { return '<th>' + h + '</th>'; }).join('') +
    '<th>KOMPARASI (DAILY VS WEEK SEBELUMNYA)</th>' +
  '</tr>';

  tBody.innerHTML = rows.map(function(r) {
    var daily0 = 0, prev0 = 0;
    var daily130 = 0, prev130 = 0;
    var daily3190 = 0, prev3190 = 0;

    var rowCells = transformedHeaders.map(function(h, cIdx) {
      var cellVal = r['_raw_' + cIdx] !== undefined ? r['_raw_' + cIdx] : (r[canonicalHeader_(h)] || '-');
      var num = window.numVal(cellVal);
      var hLower = h.toLowerCase();

      // Deteksi Nilai Daily / Aktual
      if (hLower.indexOf('dayli') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('daily') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('aktual') !== -1) {
        if (hLower.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('98%') !== -1) daily0 = num;
        else if (hLower.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('55%') !== -1) daily130 = num;
        else if (hLower.indexOf('31-90') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('13%') !== -1) daily3190 = num;
      }

      // Deteksi Nilai Week Sebelumnya
      if (hLower.indexOf('week sebelumnya') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('w1') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('w2') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('w3') !== -1) {
        if (hLower.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('98%') !== -1) prev0 = num;
        else if (hLower.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('55%') !== -1) prev130 = num;
        else if (hLower.indexOf('31-90') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('13%') !== -1) prev3190 = num;
      }

      // 2 WARNA MURNI: HIJAU & MERAH SESUAI TARGET RESMI
      if (hLower.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('98%') !== -1) {
        if (num >= 98) return '<span class="lp-target-green">' + num.toFixed(1) + '%</span>';
        else if (num > 0) return '<span class="lp-target-red">' + num.toFixed(1) + '%</span>';
      } else if (hLower.indexOf('1-30') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('55%') !== -1) {
        if (num >= 55) return '<span class="lp-target-green">' + num.toFixed(1) + '%</span>';
        else if (num > 0) return '<span class="lp-target-red">' + num.toFixed(1) + '%</span>';
      } else if (hLower.indexOf('31-90') !== -1 || hLower.indexOf('13%') !== -1) {
        if (num >= 13) return '<span class="lp-target-green">' + num.toFixed(1) + '%</span>';
        else if (num > 0) return '<span class="lp-target-red">' + num.toFixed(1) + '%</span>';
      }

      return cellVal;
    });

    // Menghitung komparasi multi-kolom Daily vs Week Sebelumnya untuk ketiga parameter
    var komparasiHtml = '-';
    var hasComparison = (daily0 > 0 && prev0 > 0) || (daily130 > 0 && prev130 > 0) || (daily3190 > 0 && prev3190 > 0);

    if (hasComparison) {
      var diff0 = daily0 - prev0;
      var diff130 = daily130 - prev130;
      var diff3190 = daily3190 - prev3190;

      var tag0 = (prev0 > 0) ? (diff0 >= 0 ? '<span class="lp-diff-text-up">▲ Naik (+' + diff0.toFixed(1) + '%)</span>' : '<span class="lp-diff-text-down">▼ Turun (' + diff0.toFixed(1) + '%)</span>') : '-';
      var tag130 = (prev130 > 0) ? (diff130 >= 0 ? '<span class="lp-diff-text-up">▲ Naik (+' + diff130.toFixed(1) + '%)</span>' : '<span class="lp-diff-text-down">▼ Turun (' + diff130.toFixed(1) + '%)</span>') : '-';
      var tag3190 = (prev3190 > 0) ? (diff3190 >= 0 ? '<span class="lp-diff-text-up">▲ Naik (+' + diff3190.toFixed(1) + '%)</span>' : '<span class="lp-diff-text-down">▼ Turun (' + diff3190.toFixed(1) + '%)</span>') : '-';

      komparasiHtml = '<div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:3px; font-size:10px; line-height:1.2; text-align:left;">' +
        '<div><strong>DPD 0:</strong> ' + tag0 + '</div>' +
        '<div><strong>1-30:</strong> ' + tag130 + '</div>' +
        '<div><strong>31-90:</strong> ' + tag3190 + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
    }

    return '<tr>' +
      rowCells.map(function(c) { return '<td>' + c + '</td>'; }).join('') +
      '<td>' + komparasiHtml + '</td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
};

window.loadPerformaKpPerWeek = function(forceRefresh) {
  var box = document.getElementById('performaKpWeekBox');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('kpWeekTableBody');
  if (!box || !tBody) return;

  box.style.display = 'block';
  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Mengkalkulasi komparasi mingguan Day-to-Day...</td></tr>';
  window.showGlobalLoader('Memuat performa mingguan KP...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getPerformaKpPerWeekSummary === 'function') {
    var fArea = document.getElementById('filterArea') ? document.getElementById('filterArea').value : '';
    var fPoint = document.getElementById('filterPoint') ? document.getElementById('filterPoint').value : '';
    var fBP = document.getElementById('filterBP') ? document.getElementById('filterBP').value : '';

    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.kpWeekData = res;
        window.renderKpWeekTable();

        if (res.gagalCairMetrics) {
          var m = res.gagalCairMetrics;
          var elPot = document.getElementById('gcValPotensi');
          var elDef = document.getElementById('gcValDefisit');
          var elPDef = document.getElementById('gcValPersenDefisit');
          var elAct = document.getElementById('gcValActual');
          var elPAct = document.getElementById('gcValPersenActual');
          var gcBox = document.getElementById('gagalCairAnalysisBox');

          if (elPot) elPot.textContent = 'Rp ' + m.totalPotensiPlafond.toLocaleString('id-ID');
          if (elDef) elDef.textContent = '- Rp ' + m.totalGagalCairNominal.toLocaleString('id-ID');
          if (elPDef) elPDef.textContent = 'Terkurang ' + m.persenTerkurangGagal + '% dari potensi plafond riil';
          if (elAct) elAct.textContent = 'Rp ' + (m.totalSudahCairNominal + m.totalMenungguCairNominal).toLocaleString('id-ID');
          if (elPAct) elPAct.textContent = 'Pencapaian Actual ' + m.persenTercapaiPotensi + '%';
          if (gcBox) gcBox.style.display = 'grid';
        }
      } else {
        tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">' + (res ? res.message : 'Gagal memuat performa KP') + '</td></tr>';
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Error: ' + err.toString() + '</td></tr>';
    }).getPerformaKpPerWeekSummary(window.currentUser, fArea, fPoint, fBP);
  }
};

window.switchKpWeekLevel = function(level) {
  window.currentKpWeekLevel = level || 'Area';
  ['Area', 'Point', 'BP'].forEach(function(l) {
    var tab = document.getElementById('tabKpWeek' + l);
    if (tab) {
      if (l === window.currentKpWeekLevel) tab.classList.add('active');
      else tab.classList.remove('active');
    }
  });
  window.renderKpWeekTable();
};

window.renderKpWeekTable = function() {
  if (!window.kpWeekData) return;
  var tHead = document.getElementById('kpWeekTableHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('kpWeekTableBody');
  if (!tHead || !tBody) return;

  var list = [];
  if (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'Point') list = window.kpWeekData.pointSummary || [];
  else if (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'BP') list = window.kpWeekData.bpSummary || [];
  else list = window.kpWeekData.areaSummary || [];

  var dayFilter = window.currentKpWeekDay || 'all';
  var days = ['Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'];
  var displayDays = (dayFilter === 'all') ? days : [dayFilter];

  var entityColLabel = (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'BP') ? 'NAMA BP' : (window.currentKpWeekLevel === 'Point') ? 'BRANCH' : 'AREA';
  var komparasiHeaderLabel = (dayFilter === 'all') ? 'KOMPARASI W2 VS W3' : ('KOMPARASI ' + dayFilter.toUpperCase() + ' (W2 VS W3)');

  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' +
    '<th>' + entityColLabel + '</th>' +
    '<th>AREA</th>' +
    '<th>TOTAL WEEK 1</th>' +
    '<th>TOTAL WEEK 2</th>' +
    displayDays.map(function(d) { return '<th>' + d.toUpperCase() + ' (W3)</th>'; }).join('') +
    '<th style="color:#059669;">TOTAL WEEK 3</th>' +
    '<th>WEEK 4 (BERJALAN)</th>' +
    '<th>' + komparasiHeaderLabel + '</th>' +
    '<th style="background:#fce7f3;color:#831843;">STATUS NAIK / TURUN</th>' +
  '</tr>';

  if (list.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Tidak ada data untuk level ' + window.currentKpWeekLevel + '.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  var grandW1 = 0, grandW2 = 0, grandW3 = 0, grandW4 = 0;
  var dayTotalsW3 = {}, dayTotalsW2 = {};
  displayDays.forEach(function(d) { dayTotalsW3[d] = 0; dayTotalsW2[d] = 0; });

  tBody.innerHTML = list.map(function(item) {
    var w1Total = item.weeks.W1 ? item.weeks.W1.total : 0;
    var w2Total = item.weeks.W2 ? item.weeks.W2.total : 0;
    var w3Total = item.weeks.W3 ? item.weeks.W3.total : 0;
    var w4Total = item.weeks.W4 ? item.weeks.W4.total : 0;

    grandW1 += w1Total;
    grandW2 += w2Total;
    grandW3 += w3Total;
    grandW4 += w4Total;

    // KOMPARASI DINAMIS: BERGERAK MENGIKUTI HARI YANG DIPILIH
    var gap = 0;
    if (dayFilter === 'all') {
      gap = w3Total - w2Total;
    } else {
      var dValW3 = item.weeks.W3 ? (item.weeks.W3[dayFilter] || 0) : 0;
      var dValW2 = item.weeks.W2 ? (item.weeks.W2[dayFilter] || 0) : 0;
      gap = dValW3 - dValW2;
    }

    displayDays.forEach(function(d) {
      var valW3 = item.weeks.W3 ? (item.weeks.W3[d] || 0) : 0;
      var valW2 = item.weeks.W2 ? (item.weeks.W2[d] || 0) : 0;
      dayTotalsW3[d] += valW3;
      dayTotalsW2[d] += valW2;
    });

    var statusText = gap >= 0 ? ('NAIK +' + Math.round(gap / 1000000) + ' jt') : ('TURUN ' + Math.round(gap / 1000000) + ' jt');
    var statusClass = gap >= 0 ? 'delta-pill-up' : 'delta-pill-down';

    return '<tr>' +
      '<td><strong>' + item.name + '</strong></td>' +
      '<td>' + item.area + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + w1Total.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + w2Total.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      displayDays.map(function(d) {
        var dayVal = item.weeks.W3 ? (item.weeks.W3[d] || 0) : 0;
        return '<td>' + dayVal.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>';
      }).join('') +
      '<td style="color:#059669;font-weight:850;">' + w3Total.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + w4Total.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td style="font-weight:750;">' + gap.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td><span class="' + statusClass + '">' + (gap >= 0 ? '📈 ' : '📉 ') + statusText + '</span></td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');

  var grandGap = 0;
  if (dayFilter === 'all') {
    grandGap = grandW3 - grandW2;
  } else {
    grandGap = (dayTotalsW3[dayFilter] || 0) - (dayTotalsW2[dayFilter] || 0);
  }

  var grandStatusText = grandGap >= 0 ? ('NAIK +' + Math.round(grandGap / 1000000) + ' jt') : ('TURUN ' + Math.round(grandGap / 1000000) + ' jt');
  var grandClass = grandGap >= 0 ? 'delta-pill-up' : 'delta-pill-down';

  tBody.innerHTML += '<tr style="background:rgba(0,0,0,0.05);font-weight:900;">' +
    '<td colspan="2">TOTAL REGIONAL</td>' +
    '<td>' + grandW1.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
    '<td>' + grandW2.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
    displayDays.map(function(d) { return '<td>' + (dayTotalsW3[d] || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>'; }).join('') +
    '<td style="color:#059669;">' + grandW3.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
    '<td>' + grandW4.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
    '<td>' + grandGap.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
    '<td><span class="' + grandClass + '">' + (grandGap >= 0 ? '📈 ' : '📉 ') + grandStatusText + '</span></td>' +
  '</tr>';
};

window.loadApprovalMatrix = function(forceRefresh, weekIndex, monthKey) {
  var box = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixBox');
  var tHead = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixBody');
  if (!box || !tHead || !tBody) return;

  box.style.display = 'block';
  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Mengkalkulasi matriks approval mitra harian...</td></tr>';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getApprovalMatrixSummary === 'function') {
    var fPoint = document.getElementById('filterPoint') ? document.getElementById('filterPoint').value : '';
    var fArea = document.getElementById('filterArea') ? document.getElementById('filterArea').value : '';
    var fBP = document.getElementById('filterBP') ? document.getElementById('filterBP').value : '';

    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res) {
        window.rawMatrixSummaryData = res;
        window.renderApprovalMatrixTable();
      }
    }).getApprovalMatrixSummary(window.currentUser, '', '', monthKey || window.currentMatrixMonthKey, weekIndex !== undefined ? weekIndex : window.currentMatrixWeekIdx, fPoint, fArea, fBP);
  }
};

window.renderApprovalMatrixTable = function() {
  if (!window.rawMatrixSummaryData) return;
  var data = window.rawMatrixSummaryData;
  var tHead = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixHead');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixBody');
  var mSelect = document.getElementById('matrixMonthSelect');
  var wSelect = document.getElementById('matrixWeekSelect');

  if (!tHead || !tBody) return;

  if (mSelect && data.months && data.months.length > 0) {
    mSelect.innerHTML = data.months.map(function(m) {
      return '<option value="' + m.key + '" ' + (m.key === data.selectedMonth ? 'selected' : '') + '>' + m.label + '</option>';
    }).join('');
  }

  if (wSelect && data.weeks && data.weeks.length > 0) {
    wSelect.innerHTML = data.weeks.map(function(w) {
      return '<option value="' + w.index + '" ' + (w.index === data.currentWeekIndex ? 'selected' : '') + '>' + w.label + '</option>';
    }).join('');
  }

  var dates = data.dates || [];

  tHead.innerHTML = '<tr>' +
    '<th>NAMA BP</th>' +
    '<th>POINT</th>' +
    '<th>AREA</th>' +
    dates.map(function(d) { return '<th>' + d.substring(0, 5) + '</th>'; }).join('') +
    '<th style="color:#dc2626;">ZONK</th>' +
    '<th style="color:#d97706;">UK 1</th>' +
    '<th style="color:#15803d;">UK ≥ 2</th>' +
    '<th style="color:#1d4ed8;">TOTAL PLAFOND</th>' +
    '<th>RATA-RATA UK / BP (HARI BERJALAN)</th>' +
    '<th>RATA-RATA PLAFOND / BP (HARI BERJALAN)</th>' +
  '</tr>';

  var rows = data.matrix || [];

  if (window.matrixStatusFilter === 'zonk') {
    rows = rows.filter(function(r) { return r.zonkCount > 0; });
  } else if (window.matrixStatusFilter === 'uk1') {
    rows = rows.filter(function(r) { return r.uk1Count > 0; });
  } else if (window.matrixStatusFilter === 'uk2') {
    rows = rows.filter(function(r) { return r.uk2Count > 0; });
  }

  if (rows.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="15" class="empty">Tidak ada data approval yang cocok.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  var activeDays = data.activeDaysSoFar || 1;

  tBody.innerHTML = rows.map(function(r) {
    var avgUkHari = r.averageUkPerHari !== undefined ? r.averageUkPerHari : ((r.totalUk / activeDays).toFixed(1));
    var avgPlfHari = r.averagePerHari !== undefined ? r.averagePerHari : Math.round(r.totalPlafond / activeDays);

    return '<tr>' +
      '<td><strong>' + r.name + '</strong></td>' +
      '<td>' + r.point + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + r.area + '</td>' +
      dates.map(function(dt) {
        var st = r.dailyStatus[dt] || 'ZONK';
        if (st.indexOf('UK >= 2') !== -1) return '<td><span class="matrix-badge-uk2">' + st + '</span></td>';
        if (st === 'UK 1') return '<td><span class="matrix-badge-uk1">UK 1</span></td>';
        return '<td><span class="matrix-badge-zonk">ZONK</span></td>';
      }).join('') +
      '<td style="color:#dc2626;font-weight:800;">' + r.zonkCount + '</td>' +
      '<td style="color:#d97706;font-weight:800;">' + r.uk1Count + '</td>' +
      '<td style="color:#15803d;font-weight:800;">' + r.uk2Count + '</td>' +
      '<td style="color:#1d4ed8;font-weight:850;">Rp ' + r.totalPlafond.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
      '<td><strong>' + avgUkHari + ' Mitra/Hari</strong></td>' +
      '<td><strong>Rp ' + avgPlfHari.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</strong></td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
};

window.filterMatrixStatus = function(status) {
  window.matrixStatusFilter = status || 'all';
  var btnReset = document.getElementById('btnResetMatrixFilter');
  if (btnReset) btnReset.style.display = (status === 'all') ? 'none' : 'inline-block';
  window.renderApprovalMatrixTable();
};

window.onMatrixMonthChange = function(mKey) {
  window.currentMatrixMonthKey = mKey;
  window.loadApprovalMatrix(false, 0, mKey);
};

window.onMatrixWeekChange = function(wIdx) {
  window.currentMatrixWeekIdx = parseInt(wIdx, 10) || 0;
  window.loadApprovalMatrix(false, window.currentMatrixWeekIdx, window.currentMatrixMonthKey);
};

  var container = document.getElementById('approvalMatrixTableContainer');
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnCollapseApprovalMatrix');
  if (!container || !btn) return;
  var isHidden = (container.style.display === 'none');
  container.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
  btn.textContent = isHidden ? '[-] Ciutkan' : '[+] Buka Matriks';
};

window.loadPerformaPencapaian = function(forceRefresh) {
  var box = document.getElementById('performaPencapaianBox');
  if (!box) return;

  box.style.display = 'block';

  var topPtBody = document.getElementById('top5PointTableBody');
  var botPtBody = document.getElementById('bottom5PointTableBody');
  var topBpBody = document.getElementById('top10BpTableBody');
  var botBpBody = document.getElementById('bottom10BpTableBody');

  if (topPtBody) topPtBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Top 5 Point...</td></tr>';
  if (botPtBody) botPtBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Bottom 5 Point...</td></tr>';
  if (topBpBody) topBpBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Top 10 BP...</td></tr>';
  if (botBpBody) botBpBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Memuat data Bottom 10 BP...</td></tr>';

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memuat data performa pencapaian...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getPerformaPencapaianSummary === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.renderPerformaPencapaian(res);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.showToast('Gagal memuat performa: ' + err.toString());
    }).getPerformaPencapaianSummary(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.renderPerformaPencapaian = function(data) {
  var topPtBody = document.getElementById('top5PointTableBody');
  var botPtBody = document.getElementById('bottom5PointTableBody');
  var topBpBody = document.getElementById('top10BpTableBody');
  var botBpBody = document.getElementById('bottom10BpTableBody');

  var renderPointRows = function(list, isTop) {
    if (!list || list.length === 0) return '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Tidak ada data point.</td></tr>';
    return list.map(function(item, idx) {
      var rColor = isTop ? '#15803d' : '#b91c1c';
      var rankNum = item.ranking || (idx + 1);

      // ZettBOT Feature: Ikon & Tooltip pergerakan peringkat Point
      var iconMove = '';
      var tooltip = item.rankTooltip || ('Peringkat #' + rankNum);
      if (item.rankChange === 'up') {
        iconMove = ' <span style="color:#15803d;font-weight:900;" title="' + tooltip + '">▲</span>';
      } else if (item.rankChange === 'down') {
        iconMove = ' <span style="color:#b91c1c;font-weight:900;" title="' + tooltip + '">▼</span>';
      } else {
        iconMove = ' <span style="color:#64748b;font-weight:800;" title="' + tooltip + '">-</span>';
      }

      var rankBadge = '<span class="badge-pill ' + (isTop ? 'success' : 'danger') + '" title="' + tooltip + '" style="cursor:help;">#' + rankNum + iconMove + '</span>';

      return '<tr>' +
        '<td>' + rankBadge + '</td>' +
        '<td><strong>' + item.point + '</strong></td>' +
        '<td>' + item.area + '</td>' +
        '<td>Rp ' + window.numVal(item.totalPlafond).toLocaleString('id-ID') + '</td>' +
        '<td style="color:' + rColor + ';font-weight:850;">' + window.numVal(item.achievmentTotal).toFixed(1) + '%</td>' +
      '</tr>';
    }).join('');
  };

  var renderBpRows = function(list, isTop) {
    if (!list || list.length === 0) return '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">Tidak ada data BP.</td></tr>';
    return list.map(function(item, idx) {
      var rColor = isTop ? '#15803d' : '#b91c1c';
      // Rank 1 dipastikan tampil murni mulai urutan 1, 2, 3...
      var rankNum = item.ranking || (idx + 1);

      // ZettBOT Feature: Ikon & Tooltip pergerakan peringkat BP
      var iconMove = '';
      var tooltip = item.rankTooltip || ('Peringkat #' + rankNum);
      if (item.rankChange === 'up') {
        iconMove = ' <span style="color:#15803d;font-weight:900;" title="' + tooltip + '">▲</span>';
      } else if (item.rankChange === 'down') {
        iconMove = ' <span style="color:#b91c1c;font-weight:900;" title="' + tooltip + '">▼</span>';
      } else {
        iconMove = ' <span style="color:#64748b;font-weight:800;" title="' + tooltip + '">-</span>';
      }

      var rankBadge = '<span class="badge-pill ' + (isTop ? 'success' : 'danger') + '" title="' + tooltip + '" style="cursor:help;">#' + rankNum + iconMove + '</span>';

      return '<tr>' +
        '<td>' + rankBadge + '</td>' +
        '<td><strong>' + item.name + '</strong></td>' +
        '<td>' + item.point + '</td>' +
        '<td>' + item.area + '</td>' +
        '<td style="color:' + rColor + ';font-weight:850;">' + window.numVal(item.achievmentTotal).toFixed(1) + '%</td>' +
      '</tr>';
    }).join('');
  };

  if (topPtBody) topPtBody.innerHTML = renderPointRows(data.top5Point, true);
  if (botPtBody) botPtBody.innerHTML = renderPointRows(data.bottom5Point, false);
  if (topBpBody) topBpBody.innerHTML = renderBpRows(data.top10BP, true);
  if (botBpBody) botBpBody.innerHTML = renderBpRows(data.bottom10BP, false);
};

  var btn = document.getElementById('btnCollapsePerformaPencapaian');
  if (!content || !btn) return;
  var isHidden = (content.style.display === 'none');
  content.style.display = isHidden ? 'grid' : 'none';
  btn.textContent = isHidden ? '[-] Ciutkan' : '[+] Buka Performa';
};

window.renderDailyBPSummary = function(list) {
  var tBody = document.getElementById('dailyBpSummaryTableBody');
  if (!tBody) return;

  if (!list || list.length === 0) {
    tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">Belum ada inputan aktivitas BP hari ini.</td></tr>';
    return;
  }

  tBody.innerHTML = list.map(function(item) {
    var sosStr = item.sosTotal > 0 ? (item.sosAman + ' Aman / ' + item.sosPalsu + ' Palsu') : '-';
    var pelStr = item.pelayananTotal > 0 ? (item.pelayananD2D + ' D2D / ' + item.pelayananKumpul + ' Kumpul / ' + item.pelayananSemi + ' Semi') : '-';
    var wlStr = item.wlTotal > 0 ? (item.wlMinat + ' Minat / ' + item.wlTidakMinat + ' Tdk') : '-';
    var doStr = item.doTotal > 0 ? (item.doMinat + ' Minat / ' + item.doTidakMinat + ' Tdk') : '-';
    var kolStr = item.kolekTotal > 0 ? ('Rp ' + item.totalCollect.toLocaleString('id-ID')) : '-';

    return '<tr>' +
      '<td><strong>' + item.bpName + '</strong></td>' +
      '<td>' + item.point + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + item.area + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + sosStr + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + pelStr + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + wlStr + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + doStr + '</td>' +
      '<td style="color:#16a34a;font-weight:750;">' + kolStr + '</td>' +
    '</tr>';
  }).join('');
};

window.renderCharts = function() {
  if (typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;
  var key = window.currentKey;
  var data = window.filteredData || [];

  if (key === 'dashboard') {
    window.renderDashboardCharts(data);
  } else if (['pencapaianArea', 'pencapaianPoint', 'pencapaian'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
    window.renderPencapaianChart(data);
  } else if (['kpiRM', 'kpiAM', 'kpiBM', 'kpi'].indexOf(key) !== -1) {
    window.renderKpiBarChart(data);
  }
};

window.renderDashboardCharts = function(data) {
  var canvasDonut = document.getElementById('donutAktivitasCanvas');
  var canvasTrend = document.getElementById('trendCanvas');
  if (!canvasDonut) return;

  var counts = { eligible: 0, doCount: 0, sos: 0, kolek: 0, val: 0, suprise: 0 };
  var dateTrendMap = {};

  data.forEach(function(r) {
    var act = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'aktivitas') || '').toLowerCase();
    if (act.indexOf('eligible') !== -1 || act.indexOf('wl') !== -1) counts.eligible++;
    else if (act.indexOf('do') !== -1) counts.doCount++;
    else if (act.indexOf('sos') !== -1 || r.is_sosialisasi) counts.sos++;
    else if (act.indexOf('validasi') !== -1) counts.val++;
    else if (act.indexOf('suprise') !== -1) counts.suprise++;
    else counts.kolek++;

    var dStr = window.getCellValue(r, 'tanggal') || r['_raw_12'] || r['_raw_0'];
    var parsedD = window.parseDateStr(dStr);
    if (parsedD && !isNaN(parsedD.getTime())) {
      var dKey = ('0' + parsedD.getDate()).slice(-2) + '/' + ('0' + (parsedD.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2);
      dateTrendMap[dKey] = (dateTrendMap[dKey] || 0) + 1;
    }
  });

  if (window.activeChartInstance) window.activeChartInstance.destroy();
  window.activeChartInstance = new Chart(canvasDonut, {
    type: 'doughnut',
    data: {
      labels: ['Kunjungan Eligible', 'Kunjungan DO', 'Sosialisasi', 'Kolektabilitas', 'Validasi UK', 'Suprise Visit'],
      datasets: [{
        data: [counts.eligible, counts.doCount, counts.sos, counts.kolek, counts.val, counts.suprise],
        backgroundColor: ['#3b82f6', '#ef4444', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#8b5cf6', '#ec4899'],
        borderWidth: 2
      }]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom', labels: { boxWidth: 12, font: { size: 10 } } } }
    }
  });

  if (canvasTrend) {
    var sortedDates = Object.keys(dateTrendMap).sort().slice(-7);
    var trendVals = sortedDates.map(function(dk) { return dateTrendMap[dk]; });

    if (window.trendChartInstance) window.trendChartInstance.destroy();
    window.trendChartInstance = new Chart(canvasTrend, {
      type: 'line',
      data: {
        labels: sortedDates,
        datasets: [{
          label: 'Jumlah Inputan',
          data: trendVals,
          borderColor: '#1d4ed8',
          backgroundColor: 'rgba(29, 78, 216, 0.08)',
          tension: 0.35,
          fill: true
        }]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, ticks: { precision: 0 } } },
        plugins: { legend: { display: false } }
      }
    });
  }
};

window.renderPencapaianChart = function(data) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('pencapaianDonutCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;

  var sumTarget = 0, sumActual = 0;
  data.forEach(function(r) {
    sumTarget += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'target') || window.getCellValue(r, 'targettotal'));
    sumActual += window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'totaldisb') || window.getCellValue(r, 'totalplafond'));
  });

  var pct = sumTarget > 0 ? ((sumActual / sumTarget) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';

  var cTar = document.getElementById('penCardTarget');
  var cAct = document.getElementById('penCardActual');
  var cPct = document.getElementById('penCardPercent');
  if (cTar) cTar.textContent = 'Rp ' + sumTarget.toLocaleString('id-ID');
  if (cAct) cAct.textContent = 'Rp ' + sumActual.toLocaleString('id-ID');
  if (cPct) cPct.textContent = pct + '%';

  if (window.pencapaianDonutChartInstance) window.pencapaianDonutChartInstance.destroy();
  window.pencapaianDonutChartInstance = new Chart(canvas, {
    type: 'doughnut',
    data: {
      labels: ['Actual Pencapaian', 'Target Belum Tercapai'],
      datasets: [{
        data: [sumActual, Math.max(0, sumTarget - sumActual)],
        backgroundColor: ['#16a34a', '#1d4ed8'],
        borderWidth: 2
      }]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } }
    }
  });
};

window.renderKpiBarChart = function(data) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('kpiBarCanvas');
  var titleEl = document.getElementById('kpiChartTitle');
  if (!canvas) return;

  var roleLabel = (window.currentKey === 'kpiRM') ? 'RM' : (window.currentKey === 'kpiAM') ? 'AM' : (window.currentKey === 'kpiBM') ? 'BM' : 'BP';
  if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = 'Grafik Performa Skor KPI per ' + roleLabel;

  var topRows = data.slice(0, 15);
  var labels = topRows.map(function(r) { return window.getCellValue(r, 'nama') || window.getCellValue(r, 'bpbm') || '-'; });

  // Helper cerdas ekstraksi SCORE murni (anti-plafond miliaran rupiah)
  function getKpiScoreClean(row, paramType) {
    for (var k in row) {
      if (k.indexOf('_raw_') === 0) continue;
      var kNorm = k.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
      if (paramType === 'new') {
        if (kNorm.indexOf('score') !== -1 && (kNorm.indexOf('new') !== -1 || kNorm.indexOf('cairnew') !== -1)) {
          var v = window.numVal(row[k]);
          if (v > 0 && v < 500) return v;
        }
      } else if (paramType === 'renewal') {
        if (kNorm.indexOf('score') !== -1 && kNorm.indexOf('renewal') !== -1) {
          var v = window.numVal(row[k]);
          if (v > 0 && v < 500) return v;
        }
      } else if (paramType === 'dpd0') {
        if (kNorm.indexOf('score') !== -1 && (kNorm.indexOf('dpd0') !== -1 || kNorm.indexOf('dpd_0') !== -1)) {
          var v = window.numVal(row[k]);
          if (v > 0 && v < 500) return v;
        }
      } else if (paramType === 'dpd130') {
        if (kNorm.indexOf('score') !== -1 && (kNorm.indexOf('130') !== -1 || kNorm.indexOf('1_30') !== -1)) {
          var v = window.numVal(row[k]);
          if (v > 0 && v < 500) return v;
        }
      } else if (paramType === 'dpd3190') {
        if (kNorm.indexOf('score') !== -1 && (kNorm.indexOf('3190') !== -1 || kNorm.indexOf('31_90') !== -1)) {
          var v = window.numVal(row[k]);
          if (v > 0 && v < 500) return v;
        }
      }
    }

    var rawIndices = [];
    if (paramType === 'new') rawIndices = [9, 10, 11];
    else if (paramType === 'renewal') rawIndices = [13, 14, 15];
    else if (paramType === 'dpd0') rawIndices = [17, 18, 19];
    else if (paramType === 'dpd130') rawIndices = [21, 22, 23];
    else if (paramType === 'dpd3190') rawIndices = [25, 26, 27];

    for (var i = 0; i < rawIndices.length; i++) {
      var rawVal = window.numVal(row['_raw_' + rawIndices[i]]);
      if (rawVal > 0 && rawVal < 500) return rawVal;
    }
    return 0;
  }

  // Mengambil SCORE murni (0 - 140) untuk RM, AM, BM, dan BP
  var scoresTotal = topRows.map(function(r) { return window.numVal(window.getCellValue(r, 'totalskorkpi')); });
  var scoresNew = topRows.map(function(r) { return getKpiScoreClean(r, 'new'); });
  var scoresRen = topRows.map(function(r) { return getKpiScoreClean(r, 'renewal'); });
  var scores0 = topRows.map(function(r) { return getKpiScoreClean(r, 'dpd0'); });
  var scores130 = topRows.map(function(r) { return getKpiScoreClean(r, 'dpd130'); });
  var scores3190 = topRows.map(function(r) { return getKpiScoreClean(r, 'dpd3190'); });

  // PENGHITUNGAN HIGHLIGHT CARD BESAR TOTAL SKOR KPI (image_caf1e7.png) + ZETTBOT FEATURE 2
  var heroContainer = document.getElementById('kpiScoreHeroContainer');
  var heroVal = document.getElementById('kpiHeroScoreVal');
  var heroBadge = document.getElementById('kpiHeroBadge');
  var heroBadgeText = document.getElementById('kpiHeroBadgeText');
  var heroBadgeIcon = document.getElementById('kpiHeroBadgeIcon');
  var heroBar = document.getElementById('kpiHeroMeterBar');
  var heroDesc = document.getElementById('kpiHeroDesc');
  var heroTitle = document.getElementById('kpiHeroTitle');

  if (heroContainer) {
    heroContainer.style.display = 'block';
    if (heroTitle) heroTitle.textContent = 'TOTAL SKOR KPI PERFORMANCE (' + roleLabel + ')';

    var lenK = topRows.length || 1;
    var sumScoreTotal = 0;
    scoresTotal.forEach(function(sc) { sumScoreTotal += sc; });
    var avgScoreTotal = (sumScoreTotal / lenK);

    if (heroVal) heroVal.textContent = avgScoreTotal.toFixed(1) + '%';

    // Grouping Skor KPI: < 80% Need Improvement, 80%-100% Achieve Expectation, >= 100% Exceed Expectation
    var catClass = 'need-improvement';
    var catText = 'Need Improvement';
    var catIcon = '⚠️';
    var catDesc = 'Total skor KPI belum mencapai target standar (<80%). Perlu pendampingan dan peningkatan pada parameter yang belum tercapai.';

    if (avgScoreTotal >= 100) {
      catClass = 'exceed-expectation';
      catText = 'Exceed Expectation';
      catIcon = '★';
      catDesc = 'Luar biasa! Total skor KPI melampaui target (>100%). Seluruh parameter kinerja terpenuhi dengan sangat memuaskan.';
    } else if (avgScoreTotal >= 80) {
      catClass = 'achieve-expectation';
      catText = 'Achieve Expectation';
      catIcon = '✓';
      catDesc = 'Bagus! Total skor KPI telah mencapai ekspektasi target (80% - 100%). Pertahankan konsistensi kinerja di lapangan.';
    }

    if (heroBadge) heroBadge.className = 'kpi-status-badge ' + catClass;
    if (heroBadgeText) heroBadgeText.textContent = catText;
    if (heroBadgeIcon) heroBadgeIcon.textContent = catIcon;
    if (heroDesc) heroDesc.textContent = catDesc;

    // ZETTBOT FEATURE 2: METERAN VISUAL ANIMASI
    if (heroBar) {
      heroBar.className = 'kpi-meter-bar ' + catClass;
      var barWidth = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (avgScoreTotal / 120) * 100));
      heroBar.style.width = barWidth + '%';
    }
  }

  // Diagram Summary Parameter KPI Belum Terpenuhi
  var cntNtb = 0, cntEtb = 0, cnt130 = 0, cnt3160 = 0, cnt6190 = 0, cntDpd0 = 0, cntAll = 0;

  data.forEach(function(r) {
    var rawParam = String(window.getCellValue(r, 'parameterbelumtercapai') || window.getCellValue(r, 'parameter_yang_belum_tercapai') || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (!rawParam || rawParam === '-' || rawParam === 'tidak ada') return;

    if (rawParam.indexOf('semua parameter') !== -1 || rawParam.indexOf('all parameter') !== -1) {
      cntAll++;
    } else {
      if (rawParam.indexOf('ntb') !== -1) cntNtb++;
      if (rawParam.indexOf('etb') !== -1) cntEtb++;
      if (rawParam.indexOf('1-30') !== -1) cnt130++;
      if (rawParam.indexOf('31-60') !== -1) cnt3160++;
      if (rawParam.indexOf('61-90') !== -1) cnt6190++;
      if (rawParam.indexOf('dpd 0') !== -1 || rawParam.indexOf('dpd0') !== -1) cntDpd0++;
    }
  });

  var elNtb = document.getElementById('kpiCardNtb');
  var elEtb = document.getElementById('kpiCardEtb');
  var el130 = document.getElementById('kpiCard130');
  var el3160 = document.getElementById('kpiCard3160');
  var el6190 = document.getElementById('kpiCard6190');
  var elDpd0 = document.getElementById('kpiCardDpd0');
  var elAll = document.getElementById('kpiCardAll');

  if (elNtb) elNtb.textContent = cntNtb + ' Personil';
  if (elEtb) elEtb.textContent = cntEtb + ' Personil';
  if (el130) el130.textContent = cnt130 + ' Personil';
  if (el3160) el3160.textContent = cnt3160 + ' Personil';
  if (el6190) el6190.textContent = cnt6190 + ' Personil';
  if (elDpd0) elDpd0.textContent = cntDpd0 + ' Personil';
  if (elAll) elAll.textContent = cntAll + ' Personil';

  if (window.kpiBarChartInstance) window.kpiBarChartInstance.destroy();
  window.kpiBarChartInstance = new Chart(canvas, {
    data: {
      labels: labels,
      datasets: [
        {
          type: 'line',
          label: 'Total Skor KPI (%)',
          data: scoresTotal,
          borderColor: '#1d4ed8',
          backgroundColor: '#1d4ed8',
          pointBackgroundColor: '#1d4ed8',
          pointBorderColor: '#ffffff',
          pointRadius: 6,
          pointHoverRadius: 8,
          borderWidth: 2.5,
          fill: false,
          tension: 0.2,
          order: 1
        },
        {
          type: 'bar',
          label: 'Score Mitra Cair New',
          data: scoresNew,
          backgroundColor: '#38bdf8',
          borderRadius: 4,
          order: 2
        },
        {
          type: 'bar',
          label: 'Score % Renewal',
          data: scoresRen,
          backgroundColor: '#10b981',
          borderRadius: 4,
          order: 2
        },
        {
          type: 'bar',
          label: 'Score Repayment DPD 0',
          data: scores0,
          backgroundColor: '#f59e0b',
          borderRadius: 4,
          order: 2
        },
        {
          type: 'bar',
          label: 'Score Repayment DPD 1-30',
          data: scores130,
          backgroundColor: '#ef4444',
          borderRadius: 4,
          order: 2
        },
        {
          type: 'bar',
          label: 'Score Repayment DPD 31-90',
          data: scores3190,
          backgroundColor: '#8b5cf6',
          borderRadius: 4,
          order: 2
        }
      ]
    },
    options: {
      responsive: true,
      maintainAspectRatio: false,
      scales: {
        y: {
          beginAtZero: true,
          suggestedMax: 140,
          ticks: { stepSize: 20 }
        }
      },
      plugins: {
        legend: {
          position: 'top',
          labels: { boxWidth: 12, font: { size: 10 } }
        }
      }
    }
  });
};

window.exportChartToPNG = function() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('donutAktivitasCanvas') || document.getElementById('pencapaianDonutCanvas') || document.getElementById('kpiBarCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var imageURI = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
  var link = document.createElement('a');
  link.download = 'Grafik_' + window.currentKey + '_' + Date.now() + '.png';
  link.href = imageURI;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
};

window.confirmDeleteHasilInputan = function(rowIndex) {
  var row = (window.filteredData || [])[rowIndex];
  if (!row) return;

  var subId = window.getCellValue(row, 'submissionid') || row['_raw_0'];
  var act = window.getCellValue(row, 'aktivitas') || 'Aktivitas';
  var mit = window.getCellValue(row, 'namamitra') || '-';

  window.showCustomConfirm(
    'Hapus Data Aktivitas',
    'Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus data "' + act + '" untuk mitra ' + mit + ' (ID: ' + subId + ')? Tindakan ini akan menghapus data langsung dari database Google Sheet.',
    function() {
      window.showGlobalLoader('Menghapus data aktivitas dari server...');
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.deleteHasilInputanRow === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          window.hideGlobalLoader();
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            window.showToast('✓ ' + res.message);
            window.loadCurrentData(true);
          } else {
            window.showLargeAlert('Gagal Hapus', (res ? res.message : 'Gagal menghapus data'), false);
          }
        }).deleteHasilInputanRow({ submissionId: subId }, window.currentUser);
      }
    }
  );
};
