window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl || function(url) {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return '';
  url = url.trim();
  if (url === '-' || url === '') return '';
  if (url.indexOf('lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/') !== -1) return url;
  if (url.indexOf('drive.google.com') !== -1 || url.indexOf('docs.google.com') !== -1) {
    var idMatch = url.match(/\/file\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,})/) || 
                  url.match(/id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,})/) || 
                  url.match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,})/);
    if (idMatch && idMatch[1]) {
      return 'https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/' + idMatch[1];
    }
  }
  return url;
};

window.showToast = window.showToast || function(msg) {
  var container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
  if (!container) return;
  var t = document.createElement('div');
  t.className = 'custom-toast';
  t.innerHTML = '<span>🔔</span><span>' + msg + '</span>';
  container.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(function() { if (t.parentNode) t.parentNode.removeChild(t); }, 3500);
};

window.showGlobalLoader = window.showGlobalLoader || function(msg) {};
window.hideGlobalLoader = window.hideGlobalLoader || function(finishMsg) {};
window.showLargeAlert = window.showLargeAlert || function(title, msg, isSuccess) {};
window.showCustomConfirm = window.showCustomConfirm || function(title, msg, callback) { if (callback) callback(); };
window.numVal = window.numVal || function(val) { return parseFloat(val) || 0; };
window.getCellValue = window.getCellValue || function(row, key) { return (row && row[key] !== undefined) ? row[key] : ''; };
window.formatWhatsAppNumber = window.formatWhatsAppNumber || function(phone) { return String(phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''); };
window.setButtonSpinner = window.setButtonSpinner || function() {};

window.loadAbsensiMasterPoints = function() {
  var sel = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  if (!sel) return;

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getMasterPoints === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(points) {
      if (points && points.length > 0) {
        window.masterPointsList = points;
        window.renderAbsenPointOptions();
      }
    }).getMasterPoints();
  } else {
    window.renderAbsenPointOptions();
  }
};

window.renderAbsenPointOptions = function() {
  var sel = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  if (!sel) return;

  var userRole = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  var userPoint = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.point || '').trim().toLowerCase() : '';
  var userArea = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.area || '').trim().toLowerCase() : '';

  var optionsHtml = '<option value="">-- Pilih Point Master --</option>';

  (window.masterPointsList || []).forEach(function(p) {
    if (userRole === '2-AM' && userArea && p.area.toLowerCase() !== userArea) return;
    if ((userRole === '3-BM' || userRole === '4-BP' || userRole === '5-BPR' || userRole === '6-BP Buffer') && userPoint && p.point.toLowerCase() !== userPoint) return;

    optionsHtml += '<option value="' + p.point + '" data-area="' + p.area + '" data-lat="' + (p.latitude || 0) + '" data-lng="' + (p.longitude || 0) + '" data-radius="' + (p.radiusMeter || 100) + '">' +
      p.point + ' (' + p.area + ' - ' + (p.radiusMeter || 100) + 'm)' +
    '</option>';
  });

  sel.innerHTML = optionsHtml;

  if ((userRole === '3-BM' || userRole === '4-BP' || userRole === '5-BPR') && sel.options.length === 2) {
    sel.selectedIndex = 1;
    window.onAbsenPointChanged();
  }
};

window.checkAbsensiUserState = function() {
  var banner = document.getElementById('absenAlreadyRecordedBanner');
  var formControls = document.getElementById('absensiFormControls');
  var toggleGroup = document.getElementById('absensiToggleGroup');
  var noticeExcluded = document.getElementById('absenExcludedNotice');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getAbsensiUserState === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res) {
        if (res.alreadyAbsen) {
          if (banner) {
            banner.innerHTML = '🛑 Anda sudah absen hari ini, Terima Kasih <strong>' + (res.userName || (window.currentUser ? window.currentUser.name : '')) + '</strong>!';
            banner.style.display = 'block';
          }
          if (formControls) formControls.style.display = 'none';
          if (toggleGroup) toggleGroup.style.display = 'none';
        } else {
          if (banner) banner.style.display = 'none';
          if (formControls) formControls.style.display = 'grid';
          if (toggleGroup) toggleGroup.style.display = 'flex';
        }

        if (res.isExcludedFromGroup) {
          if (noticeExcluded) noticeExcluded.style.display = 'block';
          var btnKel = document.getElementById('btnAbsenKelompok');
          if (btnKel) btnKel.style.display = 'none';
          window.switchAbsenType('Individu');
        } else {
          if (noticeExcluded) noticeExcluded.style.display = 'none';
          var btnKel2 = document.getElementById('btnAbsenKelompok');
          if (btnKel2) btnKel2.style.display = 'block';
        }
      }
    }).getAbsensiUserState(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.switchAbsenType = function(type) {
  window.absenType = type || 'Individu';
  var bInd = document.getElementById('btnAbsenIndividu');
  var bKel = document.getElementById('btnAbsenKelompok');
  var secInd = document.getElementById('individuFormSection');
  var secKel = document.getElementById('kelompokFormSection');

  if (type === 'Kelompok') {
    if (bKel) bKel.classList.add('active');
    if (bInd) bInd.classList.remove('active');
    if (secKel) secKel.style.display = 'block';
    if (secInd) secInd.style.display = 'none';
    window.loadKelompokMembersForPoint();
  } else {
    if (bInd) bInd.classList.add('active');
    if (bKel) bKel.classList.remove('active');
    if (secInd) secInd.style.display = 'block';
    if (secKel) secKel.style.display = 'none';
  }
};

window.onAbsenPointChanged = function() {
  var sel = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  var pointReqNotice = document.getElementById('pointRequiredNotice');
  var btnStartCam = document.getElementById('btnStartCam');
  var camFileInput = document.getElementById('cameraFileInput');

  var hasVal = sel && sel.value !== '';
  if (pointReqNotice) pointReqNotice.style.display = hasVal ? 'none' : 'flex';
  if (btnStartCam) btnStartCam.disabled = !hasVal;
  if (camFileInput) camFileInput.disabled = !hasVal;

  if (window.absenType === 'Kelompok') {
    window.loadKelompokMembersForPoint();
  }
};

window.loadKelompokMembersForPoint = function() {
  var selPt = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  var listContainer = document.getElementById('kelompokMemberList');
  if (!selPt || !listContainer) return;

  var pt = selPt.value;
  if (!pt) {
    listContainer.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;padding:15px;">Pilih Point terlebih dahulu.</div>';
    return;
  }

  listContainer.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;padding:15px;">Memuat data personil di ' + pt + '...</div>';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getBPListByPoint === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(bps) {
      if (!bps || bps.length === 0) {
        listContainer.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;padding:15px;">Tidak ada personil terdaftar di Point ' + pt + '.</div>';
        return;
      }

      listContainer.innerHTML = bps.map(function(bp) {
        return '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:7px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color);font-size:11px;">' +
          '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;cursor:pointer;margin:0;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-main);">' +
            '<input type="checkbox" class="kelompok-check-bp" data-nik="' + bp.nik + '" data-name="' + bp.name + '" data-role="' + bp.role + '" checked style="width:16px;height:16px;cursor:pointer;" />' +
            '<span>' + bp.name + ' <small style="color:var(--text-muted);">(' + (bp.role || '4-BP') + ')</small></span>' +
          '</label>' +
          '<select class="kelompok-status-select" data-nik="' + bp.nik + '" style="width:95px;height:28px;font-size:10.5px;padding:0 6px;">' +
            '<option value="Hadir" selected>🟢 Hadir</option>' +
            '<option value="Izin">🔵 Izin</option>' +
            '<option value="Sakit">🔴 Sakit</option>' +
          '</select>' +
        '</div>';
      }).join('');
    }).getBPListByPoint(pt);
  }
};

window.onAbsenStatusChange = function() {
  var sel = document.getElementById('absenStatusSelect');
  var info = document.getElementById('absenTimeWindowInfo');
  if (!sel || !info) return;

  var val = sel.value;
  if (val === 'Izin' || val === 'Sakit') {
    info.textContent = '*Status ' + val + ' dapat disubmit s/d pukul 15:30 WITA dan dibebaskan dari validasi jarak geofencing.';
    info.style.color = '#2563eb';
  } else {
    info.textContent = '*Batas Hadir Tepat Waktu: BP, BPR, BM (07:45 WITA), AM & RM (08:00 WITA). Hari Sabtu bebas jam absen.';
    info.style.color = 'var(--text-muted)';
  }
};

window.startCamera = function() {
  var video = document.getElementById('absensiVideo');
  var preview = document.getElementById('absensiPhotoPreview');
  var bStart = document.getElementById('btnStartCam');
  var bTake = document.getElementById('btnTakeCam');
  var bRetake = document.getElementById('btnRetakeCam');

  if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
    window.showToast('Browser Anda tidak mendukung kamera live. Silakan gunakan opsi upload kamera HP.');
    return;
  }

  navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: 'user', width: { ideal: 640 }, height: { ideal: 480 } } })
    .then(function(stream) {
      window.cameraStream = stream;
      if (video) {
        video.srcObject = stream;
        video.style.display = 'block';
      }
      if (preview) preview.style.display = 'none';
      if (bStart) bStart.style.display = 'none';
      if (bTake) bTake.style.display = 'inline-block';
      if (bRetake) bRetake.style.display = 'none';
    })
    .catch(function(err) {
      window.showToast('Gagal membuka kamera: ' + err.message);
    });
};

window.takePhoto = function() {
  var video = document.getElementById('absensiVideo');
  var canvas = document.getElementById('absensiCanvas');
  var preview = document.getElementById('absensiPhotoPreview');
  var bTake = document.getElementById('btnTakeCam');
  var bRetake = document.getElementById('btnRetakeCam');

  if (!video || !canvas) return;

  canvas.width = video.videoWidth || 640;
  canvas.height = video.videoHeight || 480;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  if (window.cameraStream) {
    window.cameraStream.getTracks().forEach(function(track) { track.stop(); });
  }

  video.style.display = 'none';
  window.drawAbsensiWatermarkOnCanvas(canvas);

  var base64 = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
  window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64 = base64;

  if (preview) {
    preview.src = base64;
    preview.style.display = 'block';
  }

  if (bTake) bTake.style.display = 'none';
  if (bRetake) bRetake.style.display = 'inline-block';
};

window.retakePhoto = function() {
  window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64 = '';
  var preview = document.getElementById('absensiPhotoPreview');
  if (preview) preview.style.display = 'none';
  window.startCamera();
};

window.handleFileInputPhoto = function(event) {
  var file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;

  var selPt = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  if (!selPt || !selPt.value) {
    window.showToast('Wajib pilih Point Master terlebih dahulu!');
    return;
  }

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memproses foto kamera...');

  var reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    var img = new Image();
    img.onload = function() {
      var canvas = document.getElementById('absensiCanvas');
      var preview = document.getElementById('absensiPhotoPreview');
      var video = document.getElementById('absensiVideo');
      var bRetake = document.getElementById('btnRetakeCam');

      var maxW = 1000;
      var scale = img.width > maxW ? (maxW / img.width) : 1;
      canvas.width = img.width * scale;
      canvas.height = img.height * scale;

      var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      window.drawAbsensiWatermarkOnCanvas(canvas);

      var base64 = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
      window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64 = base64;

      window.hideGlobalLoader();

      if (video) video.style.display = 'none';
      if (preview) {
        preview.src = base64;
        preview.style.display = 'block';
      }
      if (bRetake) bRetake.style.display = 'inline-block';
    };
    img.src = e.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
};

window.drawAbsensiWatermarkOnCanvas = function(canvas) {
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var w = canvas.width;
  var h = canvas.height;

  var selPt = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  var pointName = selPt ? selPt.value : (window.currentUser ? window.currentUser.point : '-');

  var now = new Date();
  var days = ['Minggu', 'Senin', 'Selasa', 'Rabu', 'Kamis', 'Jumat', 'Sabtu'];
  var dayName = days[now.getDay()];
  var dateStr = ('0' + now.getDate()).slice(-2) + '/' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '/' + now.getFullYear();
  var timeStr = ('0' + now.getHours()).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + now.getMinutes()).slice(-2) + ':' + ('0' + now.getSeconds()).slice(-2);

  var radius = 100;
  if (selPt && selPt.selectedIndex > 0) {
    var opt = selPt.options[selPt.selectedIndex];
    radius = opt.getAttribute('data-radius') || 100;
  }
  var dist = window.currentDistanceMeters || 0;

  var barH = Math.max(68, Math.round(h * 0.16));
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.72)';
  ctx.fillRect(0, h - barH, w, barH);

  var fontSize = Math.max(12, Math.round(w * 0.027));
  ctx.font = 'bold ' + fontSize + 'px Inter, system-ui, sans-serif';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  var lineSpacing = Math.round(barH / 3.4);
  var startY = (h - barH) + Math.round(lineSpacing * 0.9);
  var paddingLeft = Math.round(w * 0.04);

  // Baris 1: Point (Cyan)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00ffff';
  ctx.fillText('Point : ' + pointName, paddingLeft, startY);

  // Baris 2: Waktu (Kuning)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fde047';
  ctx.fillText('Waktu : ' + dayName + ', ' + dateStr + ' ' + timeStr + ' WITA', paddingLeft, startY + lineSpacing);

  // Baris 3: Lokasi & Radius (Putih)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  ctx.fillText('Lokasi : Gorontalo (Radius: ' + radius + 'm, Jarak: ' + dist + 'm)', paddingLeft, startY + (lineSpacing * 2));
};

window.calculateDistanceMeters = function(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
  var R = 6371000;
  var dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;
  var dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;
  var a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) + Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2);
  var c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
  return R * c;
};

window.getGpsLocation = function() {
  var badge = document.getElementById('geoStatusBadge');
  if (badge) {
    badge.className = 'geo-status-badge';
    badge.style.background = '#e0f2fe';
    badge.style.color = '#0369a1';
    badge.textContent = '⏳ Membaca koordinat satelit GPS...';
  }

  if (!navigator.geolocation) {
    if (badge) {
      badge.className = 'geo-status-badge danger';
      badge.textContent = '⚠️ Browser tidak mendukung GPS Geolocation';
    }
    return;
  }

  navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
    function(pos) {
      window.currentLat = pos.coords.latitude;
      window.currentLng = pos.coords.longitude;

      var selPt = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
      var targetLat = 0, targetLng = 0, maxRadius = 100;

      if (selPt && selPt.selectedIndex > 0) {
        var opt = selPt.options[selPt.selectedIndex];
        targetLat = parseFloat(opt.getAttribute('data-lat')) || 0;
        targetLng = parseFloat(opt.getAttribute('data-lng')) || 0;
        maxRadius = parseInt(opt.getAttribute('data-radius'), 10) || 100;
      }

      if (targetLat && targetLng) {
        window.currentDistanceMeters = Math.round(window.calculateDistanceMeters(window.currentLat, window.currentLng, targetLat, targetLng));
      } else {
        window.currentDistanceMeters = 0;
      }

      var isInside = (window.currentDistanceMeters <= maxRadius);
      if (badge) {
        if (isInside) {
          badge.className = 'geo-status-badge success';
          badge.textContent = '✓ Lokasi Terverifikasi (Jarak ' + window.currentDistanceMeters + 'm dari max ' + maxRadius + 'm)';
        } else {
          badge.className = 'geo-status-badge danger';
          badge.textContent = '⚠️ Di Luar Radius (' + window.currentDistanceMeters + 'm dari max ' + maxRadius + 'm)';
        }
      }

      var canvas = document.getElementById('absensiCanvas');
      var preview = document.getElementById('absensiPhotoPreview');
      if (window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64 && canvas && preview) {
        window.drawAbsensiWatermarkOnCanvas(canvas);
        window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64 = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
        preview.src = window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64;
      }
    },
    function(err) {
      if (badge) {
        badge.className = 'geo-status-badge danger';
        badge.textContent = '⚠️ Gagal membaca GPS: ' + err.message + '. Pastikan izin GPS aktif!';
      }
    },
    { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 15000, maximumAge: 0 }
  );
};

window.submitAbsensiForm = function() {
  var selPt = document.getElementById('absenPointSelect');
  var selSt = document.getElementById('absenStatusSelect');
  var remInp = document.getElementById('absenRemarksInput');

  if (!selPt || !selPt.value) {
    window.showToast('Wajib pilih Point Master lokasi absensi Anda!');
    return;
  }

  if (!window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64) {
    window.showToast('Wajib ambil atau lampirkan foto dokumentasi presensi!');
    return;
  }

  var inputStatus = selSt ? selSt.value : 'Hadir';
  var isIzinSakit = (inputStatus === 'Izin' || inputStatus === 'Sakit');

  if (!isIzinSakit && window.currentDistanceMeters > 0) {
    var opt = selPt.options[selPt.selectedIndex];
    var maxRadius = opt ? (parseInt(opt.getAttribute('data-radius'), 10) || 100) : 100;
    if (window.currentDistanceMeters > maxRadius) {
      window.showLargeAlert('Di Luar Radius Geofencing', 'Anda berada di luar radius point ' + selPt.value + ' (' + window.currentDistanceMeters + 'm dari max ' + maxRadius + 'm). Absensi Hadir tidak diizinkan!', false);
      return;
    }
  }

  var payload = {
    type: window.absenType,
    point: selPt.value,
    statusAbsen: inputStatus,
    keterangan: remInp ? remInp.value : '',
    photoBase64: window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64,
    latitude: window.currentLat,
    longitude: window.currentLng
  };

  if (window.absenType === 'Kelompok') {
    var checks = document.querySelectorAll('.kelompok-check-bp:checked');
    if (checks.length === 0) {
      window.showToast('Pilih minimal 1 anggota kelompok untuk absensi!');
      return;
    }

    var groupMembers = [];
    checks.forEach(function(chk) {
      var nik = chk.getAttribute('data-nik');
      var name = chk.getAttribute('data-name');
      var role = chk.getAttribute('data-role');
      var stSelect = document.querySelector('.kelompok-status-select[data-nik="' + nik + '"]');
      var stVal = stSelect ? stSelect.value : 'Hadir';
      groupMembers.push({ nik: nik, name: name, role: role, statusAbsen: stVal });
    });

    payload.groupList = groupMembers;
  }

  window.showGlobalLoader('Menyimpan presensi & mengunggah foto...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitAbsensi', true, '<span>💾 Submit & Simpan Absensi</span>', 'Menyimpan...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.submitAbsensi === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitAbsensi', false, '<span>💾 Submit & Simpan Absensi</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.lastGeneratedWaText = res.waText || '';
        window.openWaShareModal(res.photoUrl, res.waText);
        window.checkAbsensiUserState();
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Absensi Ditolak', (res ? res.message : 'Terjadi kendala saat menyimpan absensi.'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitAbsensi', false, '<span>💾 Submit & Simpan Absensi</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Gagal', err.toString(), false);
    }).submitAbsensi(payload, window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.openWaShareModal = function(photoUrl, waText) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('waShareModal');
  var imgPrev = document.getElementById('waModalPhotoPreview');
  var textPrev = document.getElementById('waTextPreview');
  if (!modal) return;

  if (imgPrev) {
    imgPrev.src = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(photoUrl) || window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64;
  }
  if (textPrev) {
    textPrev.textContent = waText || window.lastGeneratedWaText || '';
  }

  modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeWaShareModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('waShareModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.shareToWhatsappWithPhoto = function() {
  var text = window.lastGeneratedWaText || '';
  var waUrl = 'https://api.whatsapp.com/send?text=' + encodeURIComponent(text);
  window.open(waUrl, '_blank');
};

window.downloadWatermarkImage = function() {
  if (!window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64) {
    window.showToast('Foto tidak tersedia untuk diunduh!');
    return;
  }
  var link = document.createElement('a');
  link.download = 'Presensi_Regional_Gorontalo_' + Date.now() + '.jpg';
  link.href = window.currentAbsensiPhotoBase64;
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
};

window.copyWaTextToClipboard = function() {
  var text = window.lastGeneratedWaText || '';
  if (!text) return;
  var tempInp = document.createElement('textarea');
  tempInp.value = text;
  document.body.appendChild(tempInp);
  tempInp.select();
  document.execCommand('copy');
  document.body.removeChild(tempInp);
  window.showToast('📋 Teks laporan WhatsApp berhasil disalin!');
};

window.setupAbsensiFilterRBAC = function() {
  var colAr = document.getElementById('lapFilterAreaCol');
  var colPt = document.getElementById('lapFilterPointCol');
  var selAr = document.getElementById('lapFilterArea');
  var selPt = document.getElementById('lapFilterPoint');

  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  var uArea = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.area || '').trim() : '';
  var uPoint = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.point || '').trim() : '';

  if (role === '1-RM' || role === 'Editor' || role === 'Admin') {
    if (colAr) colAr.style.display = 'block';
    if (colPt) colPt.style.display = 'block';
  } else if (role === '2-AM') {
    if (colAr) colAr.style.display = 'none';
    if (colPt) colPt.style.display = 'block';
  } else {
    if (colAr) colAr.style.display = 'none';
    if (colPt) colPt.style.display = 'none';
  }

  var areas = {}, points = {};
  (window.masterPointsList || []).forEach(function(p) {
    if (p.area) areas[p.area] = true;
    if (p.point) points[p.point] = true;
  });

  if (selAr) {
    selAr.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Area</option>' + Object.keys(areas).sort().map(function(a) { return '<option value="' + a + '">' + a + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (role === '2-AM' && uArea) selAr.value = uArea;
  }
  if (selPt) {
    selPt.innerHTML = '<option value="">Semua Point</option>' + Object.keys(points).sort().map(function(p) { return '<option value="' + p + '">' + p + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (role === '3-BM' && uPoint) selPt.value = uPoint;
  }
};

window.onLapAreaFilterChange = function() {
  window.loadAbsensiSummaryReport();
};

window.onLapPointFilterChange = function() {
  window.loadAbsensiSummaryReport();
};

window.loadAbsensiSummaryReport = function() {
  var tBody = document.getElementById('lapSummaryTableBody');
  if (!tBody) return;

  var sDate = document.getElementById('lapDateFrom') ? document.getElementById('lapDateFrom').value : '';
  var eDate = document.getElementById('lapDateTo') ? document.getElementById('lapDateTo').value : '';
  var selM = document.getElementById('lapMonthSelect') ? document.getElementById('lapMonthSelect').value : 'all';
  var fAr = document.getElementById('lapFilterArea') ? document.getElementById('lapFilterArea').value : '';
  var fPt = document.getElementById('lapFilterPoint') ? document.getElementById('lapFilterPoint').value : '';
  var fBP = document.getElementById('lapFilterBP') ? document.getElementById('lapFilterBP').value : '';

  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="13" class="empty">Mengkalkulasi rekapitulasi kehadiran personil...</td></tr>';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getAbsensiSummaryReport === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(list) {
      if (!list || list.length === 0) {
        tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="13" class="empty">Tidak ada data absensi yang sesuai filter.</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      tBody.innerHTML = list.map(function(item) {
        var photoBtn = (item.canViewPhoto && item.latestPhoto) ?
          '<button type="button" class="absen-photo-btn" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + item.latestPhoto + '\', \'Dokumentasi Presensi ' + item.name + '\')">📷 Foto</button>' : '-';

        // Format riwayat per hari dan keterangan eksplisit untuk lembar cetak
        var riwayatHtml = '-';
        if (item.dailyRecords && item.dailyRecords.length > 0) {
          riwayatHtml = '<div style="font-size:10px; max-width:240px; white-space:normal; line-height:1.35;">' +
            item.dailyRecords.map(function(d) {
              var badgeColor = (d.status === 'Hadir') ? '#15803d' : (d.status === 'Terlambat') ? '#b45309' : (d.status === 'Izin') ? '#0284c7' : '#b91c1c';
              var ketStr = (d.keterangan && d.keterangan !== '-') ? (' (' + d.keterangan + ')') : '';
              return '<span style="color:' + badgeColor + ';font-weight:750;">' + d.tanggal.substring(0, 5) + ': ' + d.status + '</span>' + ketStr;
            }).join('; ') +
          '</div>';
        }

        return '<tr>' +
          '<td><strong>' + item.name + '</strong></td>' +
          '<td><span class="badge-pill info">' + (item.role || 'Karyawan') + '</span></td>' +
          '<td>' + item.point + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + item.area + '</td>' +
          '<td style="color:#16a34a;font-weight:800;">' + item.hadir + '</td>' +
          '<td style="color:#d97706;font-weight:800;">' + item.terlambat + '</td>' +
          '<td style="color:#2563eb;font-weight:800;">' + item.izin + '</td>' +
          '<td style="color:#dc2626;font-weight:800;">' + item.sakit + '</td>' +
          '<td style="color:#991b1b;font-weight:850;">' + (item.tanpaKeterangan || item.alpha || 0) + '</td>' +
          '<td><strong>' + item.total + ' Hari</strong></td>' +
          '<td>' + riwayatHtml + '</td>' +
          '<td>' + photoBtn + '</td>' +
          '<td style="text-align:center;">' +
            '<button type="button" class="action-detail-btn" onclick="window.openAbsensiHistoryModal(\'' + item.nik + '\', \'' + item.name + '\')">🔍 Rincian</button>' +
          '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      }).join('');
    }).getAbsensiSummaryReport(window.currentUser, sDate, eDate, selM, fAr, fPt, fBP);
  }
};

window.openAbsensiHistoryModal = function(nik, name) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('absensiHistoryModal');
  var title = document.getElementById('historyModalTitle');
  var tBody = document.getElementById('absensiHistoryTableBody');
  if (!modal || !tBody) return;

  if (title) title.textContent = 'Riwayat Presensi ' + name + ' (' + nik + ')';
  tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">Memuat detail riwayat...</td></tr>';

  modal.style.display = 'flex';

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getAbsensiDetailByUser === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res && res.status === 'success' && res.logs && res.logs.length > 0) {
        tBody.innerHTML = res.logs.map(function(l) {
          var photoHtml = l.photoUrl ?
            '<button type="button" class="table-photo-btn" onclick="window.openPhotoViewerModal(\'' + l.photoUrl + '\', \'Dokumentasi Foto ' + l.tanggal + '\')">📷 Buka Foto</button>' : '-';

          var statusBadge = '<span class="badge-pill success">' + l.status + '</span>';
          if (l.status === 'Terlambat') statusBadge = '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#fef3c7;color:#b45309;border:1px solid #fde68a;">Terlambat</span>';
          else if (l.status === 'Izin') statusBadge = '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#e0f2fe;color:#0369a1;border:1px solid #bae6fd;">Izin</span>';
          else if (l.status === 'Sakit') statusBadge = '<span class="badge-pill danger">Sakit</span>';
          else if (l.status === 'Tanpa Keterangan' || l.isAutoAlpha) statusBadge = '<span class="badge-pill" style="background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;border:1px solid #fca5a5;">Tanpa Keterangan</span>';

          return '<tr>' +
            '<td>' + l.tanggal + ' ' + (l.waktu || '') + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + statusBadge + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + (l.tipe || 'Individu') + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + l.jarakMeter + ' meter</td>' +
            '<td>' + (l.keterangan || '-') + '</td>' +
            '<td>' + photoHtml + '</td>' +
          '</tr>';
        }).join('');
      } else {
        tBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">Belum ada riwayat absensi tercatat.</td></tr>';
      }
    }).getAbsensiDetailByUser(nik, window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.closeAbsensiHistoryModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('absensiHistoryModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.printAbsensiSummary = function() {
  var printDate = document.getElementById('printDateWatermark');
  if (printDate) {
    var now = new Date();
    printDate.textContent = 'Dicetak: ' + now.toLocaleDateString('id-ID') + ' ' + now.toLocaleTimeString('id-ID') + ' WITA';
  }
  window.print();
};

window.exportAbsensiSummaryCSV = function() {
  var tBody = document.getElementById('lapSummaryTableBody');
  if (!tBody) return;

  var rows = tBody.querySelectorAll('tr');
  if (rows.length === 0 || rows[0].classList.contains('empty')) {
    window.showToast('Tidak ada data rekap untuk diekspor!');
    return;
  }

  var csv = "\uFEFF";
  csv += '"NAMA BP / USER","ROLE","POINT","AREA","HADIR","TERLAMBAT","IZIN","SAKIT","TANPA KETERANGAN","TOTAL HARI"\r\n';

  rows.forEach(function(tr) {
    var cols = tr.querySelectorAll('td');
    if (cols.length >= 10) {
      var rowArr = [
        cols[0].innerText.trim(), cols[1].innerText.trim(), cols[2].innerText.trim(),
        cols[3].innerText.trim(), cols[4].innerText.trim(), cols[5].innerText.trim(),
        cols[6].innerText.trim(), cols[7].innerText.trim(), cols[8].innerText.trim(),
        cols[9].innerText.trim()
      ];
      csv += rowArr.map(function(c) { return '"' + c.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"'; }).join(',') + '\r\n';
    }
  });

  var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  var link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = 'Rekap_Summary_Absensi_' + Date.now() + '.csv';
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  setTimeout(function() { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); }, 200);
};

window.loadPostDisbData = function() {
  window.showGlobalLoader('Memuat data konfirmasi Post Disb...');
  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getPostDisbData === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.postDisbListRaw = res.rows || [];
        window.postDisbList = res.rows || [];
        window.postDisbMetrics = res.metrics || null;
        if (typeof window.populateFilterDropdowns === 'function') window.populateFilterDropdowns();
        if (typeof window.applyFilter === 'function') window.applyFilter();
      } else {
        window.showToast(res ? res.message : 'Gagal memuat data Post Disb');
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.showToast('Error: ' + err.toString());
    }).getPostDisbData(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.refreshPostDisbBadge = function() {
  var badge = document.getElementById('postDisbBadge');
  if (!badge) return;

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getPostDisbData === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res && res.unverifiedCount > 0) {
        badge.textContent = res.unverifiedCount;
        badge.style.display = 'inline-block';
      } else {
        badge.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }).getPostDisbData(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.openPostDisbVerifyModal = function(rowIndex) {
  var row = (window.postDisbList || [])[rowIndex];
  if (!row) return;

  window.currentVerifyingRow = row;
  window.currentProofBase64 = '';
  window.currentProofMimeType = '';
  window.currentProofFileName = '';

  var modal = document.getElementById('postDisbVerifyModal');
  var elName = document.getElementById('pvCustName');
  var elLoan = document.getElementById('pvLoanId');
  var elPlf = document.getElementById('pvPlafond');
  var elDate = document.getElementById('pvDisbDate');
  var elNote = document.getElementById('pvFormatRequirementNote');
  var elFile = document.getElementById('pvProofFile');
  if (elFile) elFile.value = '';

  if (elName) elName.textContent = row.customer_name || '-';
  if (elLoan) elLoan.textContent = (row.loan_id || '-') + ' / ' + (row.customer_number || '-');
  if (elPlf) elPlf.textContent = 'Rp ' + window.numVal(row.plafond).toLocaleString('id-ID');
  if (elDate) elDate.textContent = row.disbursement_date || '-';

  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  if (elNote) {
    if (role === '3-BM') {
      elNote.innerHTML = '<strong style="color:#dc2626;">Khusus Role 3-BM:</strong> Wajib melampirkan berkas format <strong>VIDEO</strong> (.mp4, .webm, .mov, .3gp)!';
    } else {
      elNote.innerHTML = 'Role ' + role + ': Bebas melampirkan Foto (PNG/JPG), Video, atau Rekaman Suara.';
    }
  }

  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closePostDisbVerifyModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('postDisbVerifyModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.onPostDisbStatusChoiceChange = function(statusChoice) {
  var uploadWrapper = document.getElementById('pvProofUploadWrapper');
  if (uploadWrapper) {
    uploadWrapper.style.display = (statusChoice === 'Tidak Bisa Dihubungi') ? 'none' : 'block';
  }
};

window.handleProofFileSelected = function(event) {
  var file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;

  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  var statusChoice = document.getElementById('pvStatusChoice') ? document.getElementById('pvStatusChoice').value : 'Sudah Post Disb';

  if (role === '3-BM' && statusChoice === 'Sudah Post Disb' && file.type.indexOf('video') === -1) {
    window.showToast('Peringatan: Khusus Role 3-BM WAJIB melampirkan format Video!');
  }

  window.currentProofFileName = file.name;
  window.currentProofMimeType = file.type || 'application/octet-stream';

  var reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    window.currentProofBase64 = e.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
};

window.submitPostDisbVerification = function() {
  if (!window.currentVerifyingRow) return;

  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || '').trim() : '';
  var stChoice = document.getElementById('pvStatusChoice') ? document.getElementById('pvStatusChoice').value : 'Sudah Post Disb';
  var notes = document.getElementById('pvNotesInput') ? document.getElementById('pvNotesInput').value : '';

  if (stChoice === 'Sudah Post Disb' && role === '3-BM') {
    if (!window.currentProofBase64 || window.currentProofMimeType.indexOf('video') === -1) {
      window.showLargeAlert('Bukti Wajib Video', 'Khusus Role 3-BM (Branch Manager) WAJIB mengunggah bukti video konfirmasi pencairan mitra!', false);
      return;
    }
  }

  var payload = {
    loan_id: window.currentVerifyingRow.loan_id,
    customer_number: window.currentVerifyingRow.customer_number,
    customer_name: window.currentVerifyingRow.customer_name,
    plafond: window.currentVerifyingRow.plafond,
    disbursement_date: window.currentVerifyingRow.disbursement_date,
    tanggal_approve_uk: window.currentVerifyingRow.tanggal_approve_uk,
    bp: window.currentVerifyingRow.bp,
    branch: window.currentVerifyingRow.branch,
    area: window.currentVerifyingRow.area,
    phone_no: window.currentVerifyingRow.phone_no,
    status_choice: stChoice,
    notes: notes,
    proofBase64: window.currentProofBase64,
    proofMimeType: window.currentProofMimeType,
    proofFileName: window.currentProofFileName
  };

  window.showGlobalLoader('Menyimpan verifikasi Post Disb...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitPostDisbVerify', true, '<span>💾 Simpan Verifikasi</span>', 'Menyimpan...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.verifyPostDisb === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitPostDisbVerify', false, '<span>💾 Simpan Verifikasi</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.closePostDisbVerifyModal();
        window.showToast('✓ ' + res.message);
        window.loadPostDisbData();
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'Gagal verifikasi'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitPostDisbVerify', false, '<span>💾 Simpan Verifikasi</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).verifyPostDisb(payload, window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.openUnverifyPostDisbModal = function(rowIndex) {
  var row = (window.postDisbList || [])[rowIndex];
  if (!row) return;

  window.showCustomConfirm(
    'Batalkan Verifikasi Post Disb',
    'Apakah Anda yakin ingin membatalkan status verifikasi pencairan untuk mitra ' + (row.customer_name || row.customer_number) + '?',
    function() {
      window.showGlobalLoader('Membatalkan status verifikasi...');
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.unverifyPostDisb === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          window.hideGlobalLoader();
          window.showToast('✓ ' + res.message);
          window.loadPostDisbData();
        }).unverifyPostDisb({
          loan_id: row.loan_id,
          customer_number: row.customer_number,
          customer_name: row.customer_name
        }, window.currentUser);
      }
    }
  );
};

window.openContactMitraModal = function(rowIndex) {
  var row = (window.currentKey === 'postDisb') ? (window.postDisbList || [])[rowIndex] : (window.filteredData || [])[rowIndex];
  if (!row) return;

  window.activeContactMitra = row;
  var modal = document.getElementById('contactMitraModal');
  var elName = document.getElementById('contactMitraName');
  var elPhone = document.getElementById('contactMitraPhone');
  var elPlf = document.getElementById('contactMitraPlafond');

  var cName = window.getCellValue(row, 'customer_name') || window.getCellValue(row, 'namamitra') || '-';
  var phone = window.getCellValue(row, 'phone_no') || window.getCellValue(row, 'nohp') || '-';
  var plf = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(row, 'plafond') || window.getCellValue(row, 'principal_amount'));

  if (elName) elName.textContent = cName;
  if (elPhone) elPhone.textContent = phone;
  if (elPlf) elPlf.textContent = 'Rp ' + plf.toLocaleString('id-ID');

  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeContactMitraModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('contactMitraModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.actionContactMitraWA = function() {
  if (!window.activeContactMitra) return;
  var row = window.activeContactMitra;

  var phone = window.getCellValue(row, 'phone_no') || window.getCellValue(row, 'nohp');
  var cleanPhone = window.formatWhatsAppNumber(phone);
  if (!cleanPhone) {
    window.showToast('Nomor telepon mitra tidak valid untuk WhatsApp!');
    return;
  }

  var cName = window.getCellValue(row, 'customer_name') || window.getCellValue(row, 'namamitra') || 'Ibu/Bapak';
  var cNum = window.getCellValue(row, 'customer_number') || window.getCellValue(row, 'customernumber') || '-';
  var plf = window.numVal(window.getCellValue(row, 'plafond') || window.getCellValue(row, 'principal_amount'));
  var role = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.role || 'Pimpinan') : 'Pimpinan';
  var bp = window.getCellValue(row, 'bp') || window.getCellValue(row, 'bpbm') || 'petugas kami';
  var bm = row.bm_name || window.getCellValue(row, 'bm_name') || 'Kepala Cabang';

  // Template Pesan WA Post Disb dengan Konfirmasi Kunjungan / VC BM
  var waMessage = 'Halo Ibu ' + cName + ', perkenalkan saya dari ' + role + ' Amartha Regional Gorontalo. Ingin konfirmasi mengenai pembiayaan Anda (' + cNum + ') sebesar Rp ' + plf.toLocaleString('id-ID') + '. Apakah dana sudah diterima dengan baik? Dan Apakah ada ' + bp + ' meminta uang bensin, Rokok atau Uang lainnya? Dan Apakah Kepala Cabang ' + bm + ' sudah kunjungan ke rumah ibu atau melalui Video Call? Terima Kasih.';

  var waUrl = 'https://wa.me/' + cleanPhone + '?text=' + encodeURIComponent(waMessage);
  window.open(waUrl, '_blank');
};

window.actionContactMitraCall = function() {
  if (!window.activeContactMitra) return;
  var phone = window.getCellValue(window.activeContactMitra, 'phone_no') || window.getCellValue(window.activeContactMitra, 'nohp');
  if (phone) {
    window.location.href = 'tel:' + phone;
  } else {
    window.showToast('Nomor telepon mitra tidak tersedia!');
  }
};

window.initTrackingMap = function() {
  var mapBox = document.getElementById('trackingMapBox');
  if (!mapBox) return;

  mapBox.style.display = 'block';

  if (!window.trackingMap && typeof L !== 'undefined') {
    window.trackingMap = L.map('mapContainer', {
      zoomControl: true,
      attributionControl: false
    }).setView([0.5401, 123.0645], 11);

    var tileUrl = window.isSatelliteLayer ?
      'https://mt1.google.com/vt/lyrs=y&x={x}&y={y}&z={z}' :
      'https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png';

    window.currentActiveTileLayer = L.tileLayer(tileUrl, { maxZoom: 20 }).addTo(window.trackingMap);
  }

  // Pemicu invalidateSize berurutan agar peta tidak blank putih
  setTimeout(function() { if (window.trackingMap) window.trackingMap.invalidateSize(); }, 50);
  setTimeout(function() { if (window.trackingMap) window.trackingMap.invalidateSize(); }, 200);
  setTimeout(function() { if (window.trackingMap) window.trackingMap.invalidateSize(); }, 400);

  window.loadTrackingData();
  if (window.trackingInterval) clearInterval(window.trackingInterval);
  window.trackingInterval = setInterval(window.loadTrackingData, 30000);
};

window.loadTrackingData = function() {
  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getTrackingData === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(users) {
      window.rawTrackingData = users || [];
      window.renderTrackingMarkers();
    }).getTrackingData(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.renderTrackingMarkers = function() {
  if (!window.trackingMap || typeof L === 'undefined') return;

  (window.trackingMarkers || []).forEach(function(m) { window.trackingMap.removeLayer(m); });
  (window.trackingPolylines || []).forEach(function(p) { window.trackingMap.removeLayer(p); });
  window.trackingMarkers = [];
  window.trackingPolylines = [];

  var selBP = document.getElementById('trackingBpSelect');
  var targetFilter = selBP ? selBP.value : 'all';

  var users = window.rawTrackingData || [];
  var cAktif = 0, cDelay = 0, cOffline = 0;

  users.forEach(function(u) {
    if (u.diffMins < 5) cAktif++;
    else if (u.diffMins <= 30) cDelay++;
    else cOffline++;
  });

  var elAktif = document.getElementById('countAktif');
  var elDelay = document.getElementById('countDelay');
  var elOffline = document.getElementById('countOffline');
  if (elAktif) elAktif.textContent = cAktif;
  if (elDelay) elDelay.textContent = cDelay;
  if (elOffline) elOffline.textContent = cOffline;

  if (selBP && selBP.options.length <= 1) {
    var optHtml = '<option value="all">🌐 Tampilkan Semua BP</option>';
    users.forEach(function(u) {
      optHtml += '<option value="' + u.nik + '">' + u.name + ' (' + u.point + ')</option>';
    });
    selBP.innerHTML = optHtml;
  }

  var filteredUsers = (targetFilter === 'all') ? users : users.filter(function(u) { return u.nik === targetFilter; });

  var bounds = [];
  filteredUsers.forEach(function(u) {
    if (!u.lastLat || !u.lastLng) return;

    var iconHtml = '<div style="background:' + u.statusColor + ';width:18px;height:18px;border-radius:50%;border:3px solid #ffffff;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);"></div>';
    var customIcon = L.divIcon({ html: iconHtml, className: 'leaflet-marker-bp', iconSize: [18, 18] });

    var marker = L.marker([u.lastLat, u.lastLng], { icon: customIcon }).addTo(window.trackingMap);
    
    var popupContent = '<div style="font-family:Inter,sans-serif;font-size:11px;min-width:170px;">' +
      '<strong>' + u.name + '</strong> (' + u.point + ')<br>' +
      '<span style="color:' + u.statusColor + ';font-weight:750;">' + u.statusText + '</span><br>' +
      'Jarak Tempuh: <strong>' + (u.totalDistanceKm || 0) + ' km</strong><br>' +
      '<button type="button" style="background:#1d4ed8;color:white;border:none;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:5px;font-size:10px;cursor:pointer;margin-top:5px;" onclick="window.openStreetView(' + u.lastLat + ', ' + u.lastLng + ', \'' + u.name + '\')">🗺️ Street View</button>' +
    '</div>';

    marker.bindPopup(popupContent);
    window.trackingMarkers.push(marker);
    bounds.push([u.lastLat, u.lastLng]);

    if (u.history && u.history.length > 1) {
      var latLngs = u.history.map(function(h) { return [h.lat, h.lng]; });
      var polyline = L.polyline(latLngs, { color: u.statusColor, weight: 3, opacity: 0.75, dashArray: '4, 6' }).addTo(window.trackingMap);
      window.trackingPolylines.push(polyline);
    }
  });

  if (bounds.length > 0 && targetFilter !== 'all') {
    window.trackingMap.fitBounds(bounds, { maxZoom: 16, padding: [40, 40] });
  }
};

window.filterTrackingMap = function() {
  window.renderTrackingMarkers();
};

window.openStreetView = function(lat, lng, name) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('streetViewModal');
  var iframe = document.getElementById('streetViewIframe');
  var title = document.getElementById('svModalTitle');
  var coords = document.getElementById('svModalCoords');
  var btnExt = document.getElementById('btnOpenExternalStreetView');
  if (!modal || !iframe) return;

  if (title) title.textContent = 'Street View Lokasi: ' + name;
  if (coords) coords.textContent = lat + ', ' + lng;

  var svUrl = 'https://www.google.com/maps?q=&layer=c&cbll=' + lat + ',' + lng + '&cbp=11,0,0,0,0&output=svembed';
  iframe.src = svUrl;

  if (btnExt) {
    btnExt.href = 'https://www.google.com/maps?q=&layer=c&cbll=' + lat + ',' + lng;
  }

  modal.style.display = 'flex';
};

window.closeStreetViewModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('streetViewModal');
  var iframe = document.getElementById('streetViewIframe');
  if (iframe) iframe.src = '';
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.toggleAssistantModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('assistantModal');

window.handleAssistantKey = function(event) {
  if (event.key === 'Enter') {
    window.sendAssistantQuery();
  }
};

window.sendAssistantQuery = function() {
  var inp = document.getElementById('assistantInput');
  if (!inp || !inp.value.trim()) return;
  var q = inp.value.trim();
  inp.value = '';
  window.askAssistant(q);
};

window.askAssistant = function(query) {
  var body = document.getElementById('assistantBody');
  if (!body) return;

  var uMsg = document.createElement('div');
  uMsg.style.cssText = 'background:#10b981;color:white;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:10px;align-self:flex-end;max-width:85%;font-size:11.5px;';
  uMsg.textContent = query;
  body.appendChild(uMsg);

  var qLower = query.toLowerCase().trim();
  var ansHtml = '';

  var userName = window.currentUser ? window.currentUser.name : 'Rekan';
  var userRole = window.currentUser ? window.currentUser.role : 'Petugas';

  if (qLower === 'halo' || qLower === 'hai' || qLower === 'haalo' || qLower.indexOf('selamat') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '👋 Halo ' + userName + '! Senang bisa membantu Anda hari ini. Ada yang bisa saya bantu terkait input aktivitas, kendala absensi GPS, atau monitoring data lapangan Regional Gorontalo? 😊';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('sayang') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('cinta') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 Tentu saja! Sebagai AsistanAktivity, saya selalu siap mendampingi dan mendukung kelancaran pekerjaan Anda setiap hari di Regional Gorontalo! Tetap semangat ya ' + userName + '! 💪✨';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('siapa kamu') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('kamu siapa') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 Saya adalah <strong>AsistanAktivity</strong>, asisten cerdas operasional berbasis web app khusus untuk Regional Gorontalo. Saya dirancang untuk membantu menjawab SOP, link formulir, absensi, hingga komparasi performa tanpa perlu koneksi API luar.';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('apa kabar') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('gimana kabar') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 Kabar saya selalu luar biasa dan sistem server Regional Gorontalo beroperasi 100% prima! Bagaimana dengan target dan aktivitas Anda hari ini? Semoga lancar dan berkah ya!';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('capek') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('lelah') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('pusing') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '☕ Istirahat sejenak ya ' + userName + ', jangan lupa minum air putih! Perjuangan tim lapangan sangat berharga demi kemajuan Regional Gorontalo. Tarik napas dalam-dalam, lalu kita lanjutkan kembali saat Anda sudah segar! 😊';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('terima kasih') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('makasih') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('thanks') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🙏 Sama-sama ' + userName + '! Senang bisa membantu Anda. Jika butuh panduan link atau analisa angka lainnya, panggil saya kapan saja ya!';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('link') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('formulir') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('input') !== -1) {

      '• <strong>Link Inputan Sosialisasi:</strong> <a href="https://formlending.lendingformamartha.workers.dev/" target="_blank" style="color:#059669;font-weight:750;">https://formlending.lendingformamartha.workers.dev/</a>';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('sosialisasi') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 🌐 <strong>Formulir Sosialisasi Calon Mitra:</strong><br>' +

  } else if (qLower.indexOf('absen') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 📍 <strong>Panduan Absensi Geofencing Regional Gorontalo:</strong><br>' +

  } else if (qLower.indexOf('postdisb') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('post disb') !== -1) {

      '• Khusus Role <strong>3-BM</strong>: Wajib mengunggah bukti format <strong>VIDEO</strong> (.mp4/.webm/.mov).<br>' +

  } else if (qLower.indexOf('kpi') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 📊 <strong>Parameter Utama Evaluasi KPI Regional:</strong><br>' +
      '1. NTB & ETB Disbursement NOA.<br>' +

      '3. % Repayment Rate DPD 1-30 (Target 55%).<br>' +
      '4. % Repayment Rate DPD 31-90 (Target 13%).';
