    ansHtml = '🤖 📦 <strong>Modul Monitoring Group Loan (GD):</strong><br>' +
      'Memantau pengajuan Group Loan (GD 1 & GD 2), Pending, dan Dropout yang disinkronisasi langsung dengan status pencairan di sheet KP Daily.';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('performa kp') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('kp per week') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 📈 <strong>Performa KP Per Week:</strong><br>' +
      'Menampilkan analisis mingguan (W1 s/d W4) dan Day-to-Day (Senin vs Senin, dst.) per Area, Point, dan BP mencakup GAP, status NAIK/TURUN, serta analisa defisit riil penurunan akibat Gagal Cair.';
  } else if (qLower.indexOf('bahan') !== -1 || qLower.indexOf('leads') !== -1) {
    ansHtml = '🤖 📂 <strong>Bahan Kunjungan (WOB 6+ & Master Leads):</strong><br>' +
      '• <strong>WOB 6+:</strong> Data mitra DPD 7+ tersinkronisasi hasil visit WOB.<br>' +
      '• <strong>Master Leads:</strong> Data calon mitra (Renewal, Drawdown, Reactivation) dengan dukungan Drag & Drop file WPS/Excel.';
  } else {
    ansHtml = '🤖 Saya mengerti pertanyaan Anda terkait "' + query + '", ' + userName + '. Silakan gunakan salah satu tombol pintasan di bawah untuk panduan langsung seputar Tautan Aktivitas, Absensi Geofencing, Aturan Post Disb, atau Komparasi KP Per Week.';
  }

  var bMsg = document.createElement('div');
  bMsg.style.cssText = 'background:#f0fdf4;border:1px solid #bbf7d0;color:#166534;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;align-self:flex-start;max-width:88%;font-size:11.5px;line-height:1.5;';
  bMsg.innerHTML = ansHtml;
  body.appendChild(bMsg);

  body.scrollTop = body.scrollHeight;
};

window.toggleChatBox = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('chatBoxModal');
  if (!modal) return;
  var isHidden = (modal.style.display === 'none' || modal.style.display === '');
  modal.style.display = isHidden ? 'flex' : 'none';

  if (isHidden) {
    window.unreadChatCount = 0;
    var badge = document.getElementById('chatUnreadBadge');
    if (badge) badge.style.display = 'none';

    window.loadChatMessages();
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.markChatMessagesAsRead === 'function') {
      google.script.run.markChatMessagesAsRead(window.currentUser);
    }
  }
};

window.handleScopeChange = function() {
  var scopeSel = document.getElementById('chatScopeSelect');
  var targetSel = document.getElementById('chatTargetBPSelect');
  if (!scopeSel || !targetSel) return;

  if (scopeSel.value === 'direct') {
    targetSel.style.display = 'block';
    if (targetSel.options.length <= 1) {
      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getBPList === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(bps) {
          window.cachedBpList = bps || [];
          var curVal = targetSel.value;
          targetSel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih BP / User Tujuan --</option>' +
            window.cachedBpList.map(function(b) { return '<option value="' + b.nik + '">' + b.name + ' (' + b.point + ')</option>'; }).join('');
          if (curVal) targetSel.value = curVal;
        }).getBPList();
      }
    }
  } else {
    targetSel.style.display = 'none';
  }
};

window.handleChatKey = function(event) {
  if (event.key === 'Enter') {
    window.sendChatMessage();
  }
};

window.sendChatMessage = function() {
  var inp = document.getElementById('chatInput');
  var scopeSel = document.getElementById('chatScopeSelect');
  var targetSel = document.getElementById('chatTargetBPSelect');

  if (!inp || !inp.value.trim() || !window.currentUser) return;

  var msg = inp.value.trim();
  inp.value = '';

  var scope = scopeSel ? scopeSel.value : 'all';
  var targetVal = (scope === 'direct' && targetSel) ? targetSel.value : '';

  var payload = {
    senderName: window.currentUser.name,
    senderNik: window.currentUser.nik,
    senderRole: window.currentUser.role,
    senderPoint: window.currentUser.point,
    senderArea: window.currentUser.area,
    scope: scope,
    targetValue: targetVal,
    message: msg
  };

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.sendGlobalMessage === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.loadChatMessages();
      }
    }).sendGlobalMessage(payload);
  }
};

window.loadChatMessages = function() {
  var container = document.getElementById('chatMessages');
  if (!container) return;

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getGlobalChatMessages === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(msgs) {
      if (!msgs || msgs.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px;padding:20px;">Belum ada pesan di obrolan ini.</div>';
        return;
      }

      var myNik = window.currentUser ? String(window.currentUser.nik).trim() : '';

      container.innerHTML = msgs.map(function(m) {
        var isMe = (String(m.senderNik).trim() === myNik);
        var cls = isMe ? 'chat-msg outgoing' : 'chat-msg incoming';

        var scopeBadge = '';
        if (m.scope === 'direct') scopeBadge = '<span class="chat-scope-badge" style="background:#fef3c7;color:#b45309;">🔒 Privat</span>';
        else if (m.scope === 'area') scopeBadge = '<span class="chat-scope-badge area">🏢 Area</span>';
        else if (m.scope === 'point') scopeBadge = '<span class="chat-scope-badge point">📍 Point</span>';
        else scopeBadge = '<span class="chat-scope-badge pub">🌐 Publik</span>';

        var readMark = isMe ? (m.isRead ? ' <span style="font-size:9.5px;color:#86efac;">✓✓ Dibaca</span>' : ' <span style="font-size:9.5px;opacity:0.7;">✓ Terkirim</span>') : '';
        var deleteBtn = isMe ? ' <button type="button" style="border:none;background:transparent;cursor:pointer;color:#fca5a5;font-size:9px;" onclick="window.confirmDeleteChat(\'' + m.timestamp + '\', \'' + m.senderNik + '\')" title="Tarik Pesan">🗑 Tarik</button>' : '';

        return '<div class="' + cls + '">' +
          (!isMe ? '<div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#1d4ed8;margin-bottom:2px;">' + m.senderName + ' <small>(' + m.senderRole + ')</small>' + scopeBadge + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div style="font-size:11.5px;line-height:1.4;">' + m.message + '</div>' +
          '<div style="font-size:9px;opacity:0.75;text-align:right;margin-top:3px;">' + (m.timestamp ? m.timestamp.substring(11, 16) : '') + readMark + deleteBtn + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
      }).join('');

      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
    }).getGlobalChatMessages(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.confirmDeleteChat = function(ts, nik) {
  window.showCustomConfirm('Tarik Pesan', 'Apakah Anda yakin ingin menarik/menghapus pesan ini?', function() {
    if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.deleteGlobalMessage === 'function') {
      google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
        if (res && res.status === 'success') {
          window.loadChatMessages();
        }
      }).deleteGlobalMessage({ timestamp: ts, senderNik: nik });
    }
  });
};

window.openAuthModal = function(tab) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('authModal');
  if (!modal) return;
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  window.switchAuthTab(tab || 'login');
};

window.closeAuthModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('authModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.switchAuthTab = function(tab) {
  var lBtn = document.getElementById('tabLoginBtn');
  var rBtn = document.getElementById('tabRegisterBtn');
  var fBtn = document.getElementById('tabForgotBtn');
  var lForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');
  var rForm = document.getElementById('registerForm');
  var fForm = document.getElementById('forgotForm');
  var sForm = document.getElementById('settingsForm');
  var tabsHeader = document.getElementById('authTabsHeader');

  [lBtn, rBtn, fBtn].forEach(function(b) { if (b) b.classList.remove('active'); });
  [lForm, rForm, fForm, sForm].forEach(function(f) { if (f) f.style.display = 'none'; });
  if (tabsHeader) tabsHeader.style.display = 'flex';

  if (tab === 'register') {
    if (rBtn) rBtn.classList.add('active');
    if (rForm) rForm.style.display = 'block';
  } else if (tab === 'forgot') {
    if (fBtn) fBtn.classList.add('active');
    if (fForm) fForm.style.display = 'block';
  } else if (tab === 'settings') {
    if (tabsHeader) tabsHeader.style.display = 'none';
    if (sForm) sForm.style.display = 'block';
    window.populateSettingsFormData();
  } else {
    if (lBtn) lBtn.classList.add('active');
    if (lForm) lForm.style.display = 'block';
  }
};

window.handleLoginSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var nik = document.getElementById('loginNik') ? document.getElementById('loginNik').value.trim() : '';
  var pass = document.getElementById('loginPassword') ? document.getElementById('loginPassword').value.trim() : '';

  if (!nik || !pass) return;

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memverifikasi akun NIK...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnLoginSubmit', true, '<span>Masuk Akun</span>', 'Memverifikasi...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.loginUser === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnLoginSubmit', false, '<span>Masuk Akun</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success' && res.user) {
        res.user.loginTimestamp = Date.now();
        window.currentUser = res.user;
        localStorage.setItem('zettbos_user', JSON.stringify(res.user));
        window.closeAuthModal();
        window.showToast('👋 Selamat datang, ' + res.user.name + ' (' + res.user.role + ')');
        if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
        if (typeof window.openMenu === 'function') window.openMenu('dashboard');
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Login Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'NIK atau Password tidak cocok!'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnLoginSubmit', false, '<span>Masuk Akun</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Error Server', err.toString(), false);
    }).loginUser(nik, pass);
  }
};

window.handleRegisterSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var nik = document.getElementById('regNik') ? document.getElementById('regNik').value.trim() : '';
  var phone = document.getElementById('regPhone') ? document.getElementById('regPhone').value.trim() : '';
  var email = document.getElementById('regEmail') ? document.getElementById('regEmail').value.trim() : '';
  var role = document.getElementById('regRole') ? document.getElementById('regRole').value.trim() : '';
  var pass = document.getElementById('regPassword') ? document.getElementById('regPassword').value.trim() : '';

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memverifikasi NIK dengan database MPP...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitRegister', true, '<span>Daftarkan Akun & Verifikasi MPP</span>', 'Mendaftarkan...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.registerUser === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitRegister', false, '<span>Daftarkan Akun & Verifikasi MPP</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.showLargeAlert('Pendaftaran Berhasil', res.message, true);
        window.switchAuthTab('login');
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Pendaftaran Ditolak', (res ? res.message : 'Gagal verifikasi data MPP'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSubmitRegister', false, '<span>Daftarkan Akun & Verifikasi MPP</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).registerUser({ nik: nik, phone: phone, email: email, role: role, password: pass });
  }
};

window.handleForgotSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var nik = document.getElementById('forgotNik') ? document.getElementById('forgotNik').value.trim() : '';
  var contact = document.getElementById('forgotContact') ? document.getElementById('forgotContact').value.trim() : '';

  window.showGlobalLoader('Mengirim kode OTP ke email...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnSendForgotOtp', true, '<span>Kirim Kode OTP Pemulihan</span>', 'Mengirim OTP...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.forgotPassword === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSendForgotOtp', false, '<span>Kirim Kode OTP Pemulihan</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.showToast('✓ Kode OTP 6-Digit Terkirim!');
        var s1 = document.getElementById('forgotStep1');
        var s2 = document.getElementById('forgotStep2');
        var mask = document.getElementById('forgotEmailMasked');
        if (s1) s1.style.display = 'none';
        if (s2) s2.style.display = 'block';
        if (mask) mask.textContent = res.emailMasked || '-';
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'Data tidak terdaftar'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnSendForgotOtp', false, '<span>Kirim Kode OTP Pemulihan</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).forgotPassword(nik, contact);
  }
};

window.handleVerifyResetOtp = function() {
  var nik = document.getElementById('forgotNik') ? document.getElementById('forgotNik').value.trim() : '';
  var otp = document.getElementById('forgotOtpCode') ? document.getElementById('forgotOtpCode').value.trim() : '';
  var newPass = document.getElementById('forgotNewPassword') ? document.getElementById('forgotNewPassword').value.trim() : '';

  if (!otp || !newPass) {
    window.showToast('Masukkan 6-digit OTP dan kata sandi baru!');
    return;
  }

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memverifikasi OTP & mereset kata sandi...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnVerifyResetOtp', true, '<span>Verifikasi OTP & Reset Password</span>', 'Mereset...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.verifyOtpAndResetPassword === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnVerifyResetOtp', false, '<span>Verifikasi OTP & Reset Password</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.showLargeAlert('Berhasil', res.message, true);
        window.switchAuthTab('login');
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('OTP Salah', (res ? res.message : 'Kode OTP tidak valid'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnVerifyResetOtp', false, '<span>Verifikasi OTP & Reset Password</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).verifyOtpAndResetPassword({ nik: nik, otp: otp, newPassword: newPass });
  }
};

window.populateSettingsFormData = function() {
  if (!window.currentUser) return;
  var pInp = document.getElementById('setPhone');
  var eInp = document.getElementById('setEmail');
  var prev = document.getElementById('settingAvatarPreview');
  var init = document.getElementById('settingAvatarInitial');

  if (pInp) pInp.value = window.currentUser.phone || '';
  if (eInp) eInp.value = window.currentUser.email || '';

  if (window.currentUser.photoUrl && prev) {
    prev.src = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(window.currentUser.photoUrl);
    prev.style.display = 'block';
    if (init) init.style.display = 'none';
  } else if (init) {
    init.textContent = (window.currentUser.name ? window.currentUser.name.charAt(0) : 'U').toUpperCase();
    init.style.display = 'flex';
    if (prev) prev.style.display = 'none';
  }
};

window.handleProfilePhotoSelect = function(event) {
  var file = event.target.files[0];
  if (!file || !window.currentUser) return;

  window.showGlobalLoader('Mengompresi & menyimpan foto profil...');

  var reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function(e) {
    var img = new Image();
    img.onload = function() {
      var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      var maxW = 320;
      var scale = img.width > maxW ? (maxW / img.width) : 1;
      canvas.width = img.width * scale;
      canvas.height = img.height * scale;
      var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      var base64 = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);

      if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.uploadFotoProfil === 'function') {
        google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
          window.hideGlobalLoader();
          if (res && res.status === 'success') {
            window.currentUser.photoUrl = res.photoUrl;
            localStorage.setItem('zettbos_user', JSON.stringify(window.currentUser));
            if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
            window.populateSettingsFormData();
            window.showToast('✓ ' + res.message);
          } else {
            window.showToast('Gagal simpan foto profil');
          }
        }).uploadFotoProfil({ photoBase64: base64 }, window.currentUser);
      }
    };
    img.src = e.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
};

window.handleRequestProfileOtp = function() {
  window.showGlobalLoader('Mengirim kode OTP konfirmasi profil...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnRequestOtp', true, '<span>Kirim Kode OTP Verifikasi Email</span>', 'Mengirim OTP...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.requestOtpForProfileUpdate === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnRequestOtp', false, '<span>Kirim Kode OTP Verifikasi Email</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.showToast('✓ ' + res.message);
        var sec = document.getElementById('otpSection');
        var bReq = document.getElementById('btnRequestOtp');
        var bSave = document.getElementById('btnVerifySave');
        if (sec) sec.style.display = 'block';
        if (bReq) bReq.style.display = 'none';
        if (bSave) bSave.style.display = 'block';
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Gagal', (res ? res.message : 'Gagal mengirim OTP'), false);
      }
    }).requestOtpForProfileUpdate(window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.handleSettingsSubmit = function(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  var phone = document.getElementById('setPhone') ? document.getElementById('setPhone').value.trim() : '';
  var email = document.getElementById('setEmail') ? document.getElementById('setEmail').value.trim() : '';
  var oldPass = document.getElementById('setOldPassword') ? document.getElementById('setOldPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var newPass = document.getElementById('setNewPassword') ? document.getElementById('setNewPassword').value.trim() : '';
  var otp = document.getElementById('setOtpInput') ? document.getElementById('setOtpInput').value.trim() : '';

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memperbarui profil akun...');
  window.setButtonSpinner('btnVerifySave', true, '<span>Verifikasi OTP & Simpan Profil</span>', 'Menyimpan...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.updateUserProfile === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnVerifySave', false, '<span>Verifikasi OTP & Simpan Profil</span>');
      if (res && res.status === 'success') {
        window.showLargeAlert('Berhasil Diperbarui', res.message, true);
        window.currentUser.phone = phone;
        window.currentUser.email = email;
        localStorage.setItem('zettbos_user', JSON.stringify(window.currentUser));
        window.closeAuthModal();
      } else {
        window.showLargeAlert('Gagal Update', (res ? res.message : 'Gagal memperbarui profil'), false);
      }
    }).withFailureHandler(function(err) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      window.setButtonSpinner('btnVerifySave', false, '<span>Verifikasi OTP & Simpan Profil</span>');
      window.showLargeAlert('Error', err.toString(), false);
    }).updateUserProfile({ phone: phone, email: email, oldPassword: oldPass, newPassword: newPass, otp: otp }, window.currentUser);
  }
};

window.handleLogout = function() {
  window.currentUser = null;
  localStorage.removeItem('zettbos_user');
  if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
  window.showToast('🚪 Berhasil logout.');
  if (typeof window.openMenu === 'function') window.openMenu('dashboard');
};

window.toggleSidebar = function() {
  var sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  var over = document.getElementById('sidebarOverlay');
  if (!sb) return;
  var isShow = sb.classList.contains('show');
  if (isShow) {
    sb.classList.remove('show');
    if (over) over.classList.remove('show');
  } else {
    sb.classList.add('show');
    if (over) over.classList.add('show');
  }
};

window.toggleTopOnlinePopup = function() {
  var popup = document.getElementById('topOnlinePopup');
  if (!popup) return;
  var isHidden = (popup.style.display === 'none' || popup.style.display === '');
  popup.style.display = isHidden ? 'block' : 'none';
};

window.pollOnlineUsers = function() {
  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getOnlineUsers === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(users) {
      window.onlineUsersList = users || [];
      var cntEl = document.getElementById('topOnlineCount');
      if (cntEl) cntEl.textContent = (users || []).length;

      var popup = document.getElementById('topOnlinePopup');
      if (popup) {
        if (!users || users.length === 0) {
          popup.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:10px;text-align:center;color:#94a3b8;">Tidak ada user lain yang online.</div>';
          return;
        }

        popup.innerHTML = users.map(function(u) {
          var avatarHtml = u.photoUrl ?
            '<img src="' + window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(u.photoUrl) + '" class="online-user-avatar" alt="Avatar" />' :
            '<div class="online-user-avatar-initial">' + (u.name ? u.name.charAt(0) : 'U').toUpperCase() + '</div>';

          return '<div class="online-user-item" onclick="window.openUserProfileModal(\'' + u.nik + '\')">' +
            avatarHtml +
            '<div class="online-user-text-col">' +
              '<div class="online-user-name">' + u.name + '</div>' +
              '<div class="online-user-sub">' + u.role + ' • ' + (u.point || '-') + '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</div>';
        }).join('');
      }
    }).getOnlineUsers();
  }

  if (window.currentUser && typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.updateHeartbeat === 'function') {
    google.script.run.updateHeartbeat(window.currentUser, window.currentLat, window.currentLng);
  }
};

window.openUserProfileModal = function(nik) {
  var modal = document.getElementById('userProfileModal');
  if (!modal) return;

  var cachedProfile = null;
  try {
    var rawCache = localStorage.getItem('mpp_cache_' + nik);
    if (rawCache) cachedProfile = JSON.parse(rawCache);
  } catch(e) {}

  if (cachedProfile) {
    window.renderUserProfileModalData(cachedProfile);
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    return;
  }

  window.showGlobalLoader('Memuat data profil personil...');

  if (typeof google !== 'undefined' && google.script && google.script.run && typeof google.script.run.getUserMppProfile === 'function') {
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(res) {
      window.hideGlobalLoader();
      if (res && res.status === 'success' && res.profile) {
        try { localStorage.setItem('mpp_cache_' + nik, JSON.stringify(res.profile)); } catch(e) {}
        window.renderUserProfileModalData(res.profile);
        modal.style.display = 'flex';
      } else {
        window.showToast('Gagal memuat profil personil');
      }
    }).getUserMppProfile(nik);
  }
};

window.renderUserProfileModalData = function(p) {
  var elName = document.getElementById('popUserName');
  var elRole = document.getElementById('popUserRole');
  var elNik = document.getElementById('popUserNik');
  var elGen = document.getElementById('popUserGender');
  var elMasa = document.getElementById('popUserMasaKerja');
  var elSt = document.getElementById('popUserStatusKaryawan');
  var elPos = document.getElementById('popUserPosisi');
  var elPt = document.getElementById('popUserPoint');
  var elAr = document.getElementById('popUserArea');
  var elReg = document.getElementById('popUserRegional');
  var img = document.getElementById('popUserAvatar');
  var init = document.getElementById('popUserInitial');

  if (elName) elName.textContent = p.name || '-';
  if (elRole) elRole.textContent = p.posisi || p.role || '-';
  if (elNik) elNik.textContent = p.nik || '-';
  if (elGen) elGen.textContent = p.gender || '-';
  if (elMasa) elMasa.textContent = p.masaKerja || '-';
  if (elSt) elSt.textContent = p.statusKaryawan || '-';
  if (elPos) elPos.textContent = p.posisi || '-';
  if (elPt) elPt.textContent = p.point || '-';
  if (elAr) elAr.textContent = p.area || '-';
  if (elReg) elReg.textContent = p.regional || 'Gorontalo';

  if (p.photoUrl && img) {
    img.src = window.cleanGoogleDriveUrl(p.photoUrl);
    img.style.display = 'block';
    if (init) init.style.display = 'none';
  } else if (init) {
    init.textContent = (p.name ? p.name.charAt(0) : 'U').toUpperCase();
    init.style.display = 'flex';
    if (img) img.style.display = 'none';
  }
};

window.closeUserProfileModal = function() {
  var modal = document.getElementById('userProfileModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
};

window.directMessageToUser = function() {
  var elNik = document.getElementById('popUserNik');
  var elName = document.getElementById('popUserName');
  var targetNik = elNik ? elNik.textContent.trim() : '';
  var targetName = elName ? elName.textContent.trim() : targetNik;

  window.closeUserProfileModal();
  window.toggleChatBox();

  var scopeSel = document.getElementById('chatScopeSelect');
  var targetSel = document.getElementById('chatTargetBPSelect');
  var chatInp = document.getElementById('chatInput');

  if (scopeSel && targetSel) {
    scopeSel.value = 'direct';
    targetSel.style.display = 'block';

    var optFound = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < targetSel.options.length; i++) {
      if (targetSel.options[i].value === targetNik) {
        optFound = true;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!optFound && targetNik) {
      var opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = targetNik;
      opt.textContent = targetName + ' (' + targetNik + ')';
      targetSel.appendChild(opt);
    }

    targetSel.value = targetNik;

    if (chatInp) {
      setTimeout(function() { chatInp.focus(); }, 150);
    }
  }
};

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  try {
    var savedUser = localStorage.getItem('zettbos_user');
    if (savedUser) {
      window.currentUser = JSON.parse(savedUser);
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  var savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('zettbos_theme');
  if (savedTheme === 'dark' && document.body) {
    document.body.classList.add('dark-mode');
  }

  // Pengambilan Lokasi GPS Otomatis Seketika Saat Aplikasi Dibuka
  if (typeof window.triggerImmediateLocationFetch === 'function') {
    window.triggerImmediateLocationFetch();
  }

  if (typeof window.updateUserUI === 'function') window.updateUserUI();
  if (typeof window.initDraggableAssistant === 'function') window.initDraggableAssistant();
  if (typeof window.openMenu === 'function') window.openMenu('dashboard');

  if (typeof window.pollOnlineUsers === 'function') {
    setInterval(window.pollOnlineUsers, 25000);
    window.pollOnlineUsers();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  var topPopup = document.getElementById('topOnlinePopup');
  var badge = document.getElementById('topOnlineBadge');
  if (topPopup && badge && !badge.contains(e.target)) {
    topPopup.style.display = 'none';
  }
});
</script>
